<script>
/* Tema dimuat paling awal supaya halaman tidak berkedip */
(function(){
var R=document.documentElement,TK="xa-theme",AK="xa-accent",OK=["mono","default","obsidian","universe"],
 META={mono:"#F1F1EF","default":"#FBF3E4",obsidian:"#0E0A18",universe:"#060A1C"},
 VARS=["--accent","--light","--blue","--c3","--on","--ic","--acc-t"],HEX=/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i;
function ls(k,v){try{if(v===undefined)return localStorage.getItem(k);if(v===null)localStorage.removeItem(k);else localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}return null}
function h2r(h){return[1,3,5].map(function(i){return parseInt(h.substr(i,2),16)})}
function r2h(a){return"#"+a.map(function(v){return Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(255,v))).toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}).join("")}
function r2s(c){var r=c[0]/255,g=c[1]/255,b=c[2]/255,mx=Math.max(r,g,b),mn=Math.min(r,g,b),l=(mx+mn)/2,h=0,s=0,d=mx-mn;
 if(d){s=l>.5?d/(2-mx-mn):d/(mx+mn);h=mx===r?(g-b)/d+(g<b?6:0):mx===g?(b-r)/d+2:(r-g)/d+4;h*=60}return[h,s,l]}
function s2r(h,s,l){h=((h%360)+360)%360;var c=(1-Math.abs(2*l-1))*s,x=c*(1-Math.abs((h/60)%2-1)),m=l-c/2,r=0,g=0,b=0;
 if(h<60){r=c;g=x}else if(h<120){r=x;g=c}else if(h<180){g=c;b=x}else if(h<240){g=x;b=c}else if(h<300){r=x;b=c}else{r=c;b=x}
 return[(r+m)*255,(g+m)*255,(b+m)*255]}
function lum(c){c=c.map(function(v){v/=255;return v<=.03928?v/12.92:Math.pow((v+.055)/1.055,2.4)});return .2126*c[0]+.7152*c[1]+.0722*c[2]}
function cr(a,b){var x=lum(a),y=lum(b);return(Math.max(x,y)+.05)/(Math.min(x,y)+.05)}
function derive(hex){var rgb=h2r(hex),q=r2s(rgb),h=q[0],s=q[1],l=q[2];
 var pas=function(hh,sa,li){return r2h(s2r(hh,sa,li))};
 var on=cr(rgb,[255,255,255])>=4.5?"#ffffff":"#1b1722";
 var t=rgb,ll=l;while(cr(t,[255,252,246])<4.5&&ll>.05){ll-=.03;t=s2r(h,s,ll)}
 var tx=r2h(t);
 return{"--accent":hex,"--light":pas(h,.9,.88),"--blue":pas(h+160,.55,.78),"--c3":pas(h+55,.95,.8),"--on":on,"--ic":tx,"--acc-t":tx}}
function apply(){var n=XT.theme(),a=XT.accent();
 R.setAttribute("data-theme",n);
 VARS.forEach(function(v){R.style.removeProperty(v)});
 if(n==="default"&&a){var d=derive(a);for(var k in d)R.style.setProperty(k,d[k])}
 var m=document.querySelector('meta[name=theme-color]');if(m)m.setAttribute("content",META[n])}
var XT=window.XT={
 theme:function(){var t=ls(TK);return OK.indexOf(t)>-1?t:"default"},
 accent:function(h){if(h===undefined){var a=ls(AK);return HEX.test(a||"")?a:null}
  if(h===null)ls(AK,null);else if(HEX.test(h))ls(AK,h.toLowerCase());apply()},
 set:function(t){if(OK.indexOf(t)>-1){ls(TK,t);apply()}}};
apply();
})();
</script>

  <button id="dkacc" type="button" data-t="profil"><b class="ib" aria-hidden="true"><svg class="i" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4"/><path d="M4 20c1-4 4-6 8-6s7 2 8 6"/></svg></b><span>Masuk</span></button>
</nav>
<div id="toast" role="status"></div>

<script>
"use strict";
/* ============ Utilitas ============ */
const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
const ico=p=>`<svg class="i" viewBox="0 0 24 24">${p}</svg>`;
const toast=m=>{const t=$("#toast");t.textContent=m;t.classList.add("on");clearTimeout(toast.t);toast.t=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove("on"),1800)};
const copy=async txt=>{try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt);toast("Tersalin")}catch{toast("Gagal menyalin, salin manual")}};
const rand=n=>{const a=new Uint32Array(1);const lim=4294967296-(4294967296%n);let x;do{crypto.getRandomValues(a);x=a[0]}while(x>=lim);return x%n};
const fmtB=n=>n<1024?n+" B":n<1048576?(n/1024).toFixed(1)+" KB":(n/1048576).toFixed(2)+" MB";

/* ============ Registri tools ============
   Tambah tools baru: push satu objek ke TOOLS.
   { id, name, desc, cat, icon (isi svg), featured?, render(el) } */
const CATS=["Semua","Utilities","downloader","Fun Tools","Experiments"];
const TOOLS=[];

/* Belum ada tools. Tambahkan dengan menghapus tanda komentar contoh ini lalu sesuaikan:
TOOLS.push({id:"contoh",name:"Nama Tools",desc:"Deskripsi singkat.",cat:"Utilities",featured:false,
icon:'<path d="M4 6h16M4 12h10M4 18h14"/>',
render(el){el.innerHTML=`<div class="panel">Isi tools di sini</div>`}});
Kategori (CATS) dipakai untuk chip filter di beranda. Helper siap pakai: toast(), copy(), rand(), fmtB(), esc(), ico(). */

/* ============ Spotify Downloader — Izzxd API ============
   Endpoint: GET https://api.izzxd.my.id/api/music/spotifyplay?query=<kata kunci>&apikey=...
   Bentuk respons tidak diasumsikan: judul, cover, dan tautan audio dibaca dari field yang benar-benar ada.
   SP_PROXY = Cloudflare Worker (spotify-worker.js, rute /dl) agar file audio bisa disimpan langsung dari browser. */
(function(){
const SP_API="https://api.izzxd.my.id/api/music/spotifyplay";
const SP_KEY="izzxd-86cpjw6";
const SP_PROXY="https://spotify.ujicobakun299.workers.dev";
/*H*/
const isUrl=v=>typeof v==="string"&&/^https?:\/\/[^\s]+$/i.test(v.trim());
const asText=v=>typeof v==="string"&&v.trim()?v.trim():(typeof v==="number"?String(v):"");
const nameOf=v=>Array.isArray(v)?v.map(nameOf).filter(Boolean).join(", "):(v&&typeof v==="object"?(asText(v.name)||asText(v.nickname)||asText(v.title)):asText(v));
const AUDIO=/\.(mp3|m4a|aac|ogg|opus|flac|wav)(\?|#|$)/i,IMG=/\.(jpe?g|png|webp|gif|svg|avif)(\?|#|$)/i;
const spHost=h=>h==="open.spotify.com"||/(^|\.)scdn\.co$/.test(h)||/(^|\.)spotifycdn\.com$/.test(h);
function walk(o,path,out,d){
 if(o==null||d>7)return;
 if(typeof o==="string"){if(isUrl(o))out.push({path:path.join(".").toLowerCase(),url:o.trim()});return}
 if(Array.isArray(o)){o.forEach((x,i)=>walk(x,path.concat(String(i)),out,d+1));return}
 if(typeof o==="object")for(const k in o)walk(o[k],path.concat(k),out,d+1);
}
function findKey(o,keys,test){
 const q=[o];let n=0;
 while(q.length&&n++<400){
  const c=q.shift();if(c==null||typeof c!=="object")continue;
  for(const k of keys){const hit=Object.keys(c).find(x=>x.toLowerCase()===k);if(hit!==undefined){const v=c[hit];if(!test||test(v))return v}}
  for(const k in c)if(c[k]&&typeof c[k]==="object")q.push(c[k]);
 }
 return undefined;
}
function downloads(item){
 const all=[];walk(item,[],all,0);
 const seen=new Set(),res=[];
 all.forEach(({path,url})=>{
  if(seen.has(url))return;
  let h="";try{h=new URL(url).hostname}catch{return}
  if(spHost(h)||IMG.test(url))return;                 // halaman Spotify, preview 30 dtk, dan gambar
  const isAudio=AUDIO.test(url);
  if(!isAudio&&/(cover|thumb|image|img|artwork|avatar|preview|icon|album|artist|lyric)/.test(path))return;
  if(!isAudio&&!/(download|audio|mp3|dl|file|stream|link|url|src|play|music|song|media)/.test(path))return;
  seen.add(url);
  const m=url.match(AUDIO);
  res.push({url,ext:m?m[1].toLowerCase():"mp3",label:/(^|[^a-z0-9])(hd|high|320|flac)([^a-z0-9]|$)/.test(path)?"Kualitas tinggi":"Lagu lengkap"});
 });
 const n={};res.forEach(r=>{n[r.label]=(n[r.label]||0)+1;if(n[r.label]>1)r.label+=" ("+n[r.label]+")"});
 return res;
}
function coverOf(item){const all=[];walk(item,[],all,0);const c=all.find(x=>IMG.test(x.url)||/(cover|thumb|image|artwork|img|poster)/.test(x.path));return c?c.url:""}
function spotifyLink(item){const all=[];walk(item,[],all,0);const c=all.find(x=>{try{const u=new URL(x.url);return u.hostname==="open.spotify.com"&&/^\/track\//.test(u.pathname)}catch{return false}});return c?c.url:""}
function items(j){
 const root=(j&&typeof j==="object"&&!Array.isArray(j))?(j.result!==undefined?j.result:(j.data!==undefined?j.data:j)):j;
 const q=[root];let n=0;
 while(q.length&&n++<300){
  const c=q.shift();if(c==null||typeof c!=="object")continue;
  if(Array.isArray(c)){
   const objs=c.filter(x=>x&&typeof x==="object"&&!Array.isArray(x));
   if(objs.length&&objs.some(x=>downloads(x).length&&findKey(x,["title","name","track","track_name","song","judul"],asText)!==undefined))return objs.slice(0,6);
  }
  for(const k in c)if(c[k]&&typeof c[k]==="object")q.push(c[k]);
 }
 return root&&typeof root==="object"&&!Array.isArray(root)?[root]:[];
}
function metaOf(item){
 const title=asText(findKey(item,["title","name","track_name","track","song","judul"],asText));
 const artist=nameOf(findKey(item,["artist","artists","artist_name","author","singer","penyanyi"],v=>v&&(typeof v==="object"||asText(v))));
 const album=nameOf(findKey(item,["album","album_name"],v=>v&&(typeof v==="object"||asText(v))));
 const dv=findKey(item,["duration","duration_ms","durasi","length"],v=>typeof v==="number"||(typeof v==="string"&&v.trim()));
 let dur="";
 if(typeof dv==="number"||(typeof dv==="string"&&/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(dv.trim()))){let s=Number(dv);if(s>1000)s=s/1000;s=Math.round(s);dur=Math.floor(s/60)+":"+String(s%60).padStart(2,"0")}
 else if(typeof dv==="string")dur=dv.trim();
 return{title,artist,album,dur,cover:coverOf(item),link:spotifyLink(item)};
}
function failure(j){
 if(!j||typeof j!=="object")return"Respons dari server tidak dikenali.";
 const bad=j.status===false||j.success===false||j.ok===false||j.error===true||(typeof j.status==="number"&&j.status>=400)||(typeof j.code==="number"&&j.code>=400)||(typeof j.status==="string"&&/^(error|fail|failed)$/i.test(j.status))||(typeof j.error==="string"&&j.error);
 if(!bad)return"";
 let msg=asText(j.message)||asText(j.msg)||asText(j.error)||asText(j.detail)||asText(j.pesan);
 if(!msg)for(const k in j){if(typeof j[k]==="string"&&j[k].trim()&&!/^(status|success|ok|code)$/i.test(k)){msg=j[k].trim();break}}
 if(/\bapi(key|-key)?\b/i.test(msg))msg="";
 return msg||"Layanan sedang bermasalah. Coba lagi beberapa saat lagi.";
}
const safeName=t=>String(t||"spotify").replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\x00-\x1f]+/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim().slice(0,90)||"spotify";
/*E*/
const DL='<path d="M12 4v10"/><path d="M8 10l4 4 4-4"/><path d="M5 19h14"/>';

TOOLS.push({
 id:"spotify-player",name:"Spotify Downloader",desc:"Cari lagu lewat judul atau artis, dengarkan, lalu unduh audionya.",cat:"downloader",featured:false,
 icon:'<path d="M4.8 9.2c4.8-1.5 10.1-.9 14.2 1.7"/><path d="M5.8 12.5c3.8-1.1 8-.6 11.2 1.4"/><path d="M6.8 15.7c2.8-.8 5.8-.4 8.2 1"/>',
 render(el){
  el.innerHTML=`<style>
.sp-sp{width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:50%;border:2.5px solid currentColor;border-right-color:transparent;animation:spspin .7s linear infinite;flex:none}
@keyframes spspin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.sp-sp{animation-duration:2s}}
.sp-load{display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:center;border:1px dashed var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:14px;color:var(--mute);font-size:14px}
.sp-load .sp-sp{color:var(--accent)}
.sp-go{width:100%;justify-content:center;gap:8px}.sp-go[disabled]{opacity:.65;cursor:progress}
.sp-list{display:grid;gap:14px;margin-top:4px}
.sp-card{border:2px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;padding:12px;display:grid;gap:10px}
.sp-top{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:center}
.sp-th{flex:none;width:72px;height:72px;border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden;background:var(--light);display:grid;place-items:center;color:var(--ic);border:1px solid var(--line)}
.sp-th img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block}
.sp-inf{min-width:0;display:grid;gap:3px}
.sp-ti{font-weight:700;line-height:1.3;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.sp-sub{color:var(--mute);font-size:13.5px;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.sp-op{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;background:var(--bg);border-radius:14px;padding:10px 10px 10px 14px}
.sp-op b{display:block;font-size:15px}.sp-op small{color:var(--mute);font-size:12.5px}
.sp-op .btn{gap:6px;padding:10px 14px;flex:none}.sp-op .btn svg{width:18px;height:18px}
.sp-au{width:100%;height:40px}
.sp-dbg{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--mute);margin-top:10px}
.sp-dbg summary{cursor:pointer;min-height:32px;display:flex;align-items:center}
.sp-dbg pre{max-height:220px;overflow:auto;background:var(--bg);border-radius:10px;padding:10px;font-size:12px;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all;margin-top:6px}
@media(max-width:380px){.sp-op{flex-direction:column;align-items:stretch}.sp-op .btn{justify-content:center}}
</style>
<div class="panel">
 <div class="f">
  <label for="sp-q">Judul lagu atau artis</label>
  <input id="sp-q" type="text" placeholder="contoh: Nadin Amizah Bertaut" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" maxlength="200">
 </div>
 <button class="btn sp-go" id="sp-go" type="button">${ico(DL)}<span>Cari &amp; Download</span></button>
 <div id="sp-msg" role="status" aria-live="polite" class="hint">Ketik judul lagu (boleh sertai nama artis), lalu tekan Cari &amp; Download.</div>
 <div id="sp-out"></div>
</div>`;
  const $$=s=>el.querySelector(s);
  const input=$$("#sp-q"),go=$$("#sp-go"),msg=$$("#sp-msg"),out=$$("#sp-out");
  let ctl=null,seq=0;
  const say=(t,bad)=>{msg.className=bad?"err":"hint";msg.textContent=t};
  const busy=b=>{go.disabled=b;go.querySelector("span").textContent=b?"Mencari…":"Cari & Download";input.readOnly=b};
  const blobOf=async u=>{const r=await fetch(u);if(!r.ok)throw 0;const b=await r.blob();if(!b.size)throw 0;return b};

  async function save(opt,btn,name){
   const lab=btn.querySelector("span"),old=lab.textContent;
   btn.disabled=true;lab.textContent="Mengunduh…";
   try{
    let blob=null;
    const px=SP_PROXY.replace(/\/+$/,"");
    if(px){try{blob=await blobOf(px+"/dl?u="+encodeURIComponent(opt.url))}catch{}}
    if(!blob){try{blob=await blobOf(opt.url)}catch{}}
    if(blob){
     const a=document.createElement("a");a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download=name+"."+opt.ext;
     document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),4000);
     toast("Unduhan dimulai");
    }else{
     window.open(opt.url,"_blank","noopener,noreferrer");
     toast("Dibuka di tab baru. Gunakan menu pemutar untuk menyimpan.");
    }
   }finally{btn.disabled=false;lab.textContent=old}
  }

  function card(item){
   const m=metaOf(item),dls=downloads(item);
   const c=document.createElement("div");c.className="sp-card";
   const top=document.createElement("div");top.className="sp-top";
   const th=document.createElement("div");th.className="sp-th";
   if(m.cover){const im=new Image();im.alt="Cover lagu";im.referrerPolicy="no-referrer";im.loading="lazy";im.onerror=()=>{th.innerHTML=ico(DL)};im.src=m.cover;th.appendChild(im)}else th.innerHTML=ico(DL);
   const inf=document.createElement("div");inf.className="sp-inf";
   const t=document.createElement("div");t.className="sp-ti";t.textContent=m.title||"Lagu Spotify";inf.appendChild(t);
   const sub=[m.artist,m.album,m.dur].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
   if(sub){const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="sp-sub";s.textContent=sub;inf.appendChild(s)}
   if(m.link){const a=document.createElement("a");a.href=m.link;a.target="_blank";a.rel="noopener noreferrer";a.className="sp-sub";a.textContent="Buka di Spotify";inf.appendChild(a)}
   top.append(th,inf);c.appendChild(top);
   const fname=safeName((m.artist?m.artist+" - ":"")+(m.title||"spotify"));
   if(!dls.length){const n=document.createElement("div");n.className="hint";n.textContent="Tidak ada tautan audio untuk hasil ini.";c.appendChild(n);return c}
   dls.forEach(o=>{
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="sp-op";
    const d=document.createElement("div");const b=document.createElement("b");b.textContent=o.label;const s=document.createElement("small");s.textContent=o.ext.toUpperCase();d.append(b,s);
    const btn=document.createElement("button");btn.type="button";btn.className="btn";btn.innerHTML=ico(DL)+"<span>Unduh</span>";
    btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Unduh "+(m.title||"lagu")+" "+o.label);
    btn.addEventListener("click",()=>save(o,btn,fname));
    row.append(d,btn);c.appendChild(row);
   });
   const au=document.createElement("audio");au.className="sp-au";au.controls=true;au.preload="none";au.src=dls[0].url;au.setAttribute("aria-label","Putar "+(m.title||"lagu"));
   c.appendChild(au);
   return c;
  }

  async function run(){
   if(go.disabled)return;
   const q=input.value.trim();
   out.replaceChildren();
   if(!q){say("Ketik judul lagu atau nama artis terlebih dahulu.",true);input.focus();return}
   if(q.length<2){say("Kata kunci terlalu pendek.",true);input.focus();return}
   if(ctl)ctl.abort();ctl=new AbortController();
   const my=++seq,timer=setTimeout(()=>ctl.abort(),45000),signal=ctl.signal;
   busy(true);say("Mencari lagu…");
   out.innerHTML='<div class="sp-load" role="status"><span class="sp-sp" aria-hidden="true"></span><span>Sedang mencari dan menyiapkan lagu, mohon tunggu sebentar…</span></div>';
   let j=null;
   try{
    const res=await fetch(SP_API+"?"+new URLSearchParams({query:q,apikey:SP_KEY}),{signal});
    let txt="";try{txt=await res.text();j=JSON.parse(txt)}catch{}
    if(my!==seq)return;
    if(!res.ok){throw Object.assign(new Error(failure(j)||("Server mengembalikan status "+res.status+".")),{shown:true})}
    if(j===null)throw Object.assign(new Error("Respons server bukan JSON yang valid."),{shown:true});
    const f=failure(j);if(f)throw Object.assign(new Error(f),{shown:true});
    const list=items(j),withDl=list.filter(x=>downloads(x).length);
    if(!withDl.length)throw Object.assign(new Error("Lagu ditemukan, tetapi file audionya belum tersedia. Coba kata kunci lain."),{shown:true});
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.className="sp-list";
    withDl.forEach(x=>wrap.appendChild(card(x)));
    out.replaceChildren(wrap);
    say(withDl.length>1?"Ditemukan "+withDl.length+" hasil. Pilih lagu yang kamu mau.":"Lagu ditemukan. Putar atau unduh di bawah.");
   }catch(e){
    if(my!==seq)return;
    out.replaceChildren();
    let t;
    if(e&&e.shown)t=e.message;
    else if(signal.aborted||(e&&e.name==="AbortError"))t="Server terlalu lama merespons. Coba lagi beberapa saat lagi.";
    else if(e instanceof TypeError)t="Tidak dapat terhubung ke server. Periksa koneksi internet kamu lalu coba lagi.";
    else t="Terjadi kesalahan tak terduga. Coba lagi.";
    say(t,true);
    console.error("Spotify Downloader:",e);
   }finally{clearTimeout(timer);if(my===seq)busy(false)}
  }
  go.addEventListener("click",run);
  input.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter")run()});
 }
});
})();

 render(el){

    .qr-player{border:2px solid var(--bd);border-radius:16px;padding:14px;background:var(--blue)}
    .qr-player-top{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap}
    .qr-status{font-size:13px;color:var(--mute);margin-top:8px}
    .qr-list{display:grid;gap:10px}
    .qr-ayah{border:1.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--card);padding:14px;display:grid;gap:9px}
    .qr-ayah-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;color:var(--mute);font-size:12px;font-weight:800}
    .qr-ar{font-family:"Noto Naskh Arabic","Amiri","Traditional Arabic",serif;font-size:clamp(25px,6vw,34px);line-height:2.05;text-align:right;direction:rtl;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
    .qr-latin{font-size:14px;color:var(--acc-t);line-height:1.7}
    .qr-translation{font-size:15px;line-height:1.65}
    .qr-note{font-size:13px;color:var(--mute);line-height:1.5}
    .qr-empty{padding:18px;border:2px dashed var(--line);border-radius:14px;text-align:center;color:var(--mute)}
    @media(min-width:640px){.qr-fields{grid-template-columns:1.25fr 1fr}}
   </style>
   <div class="qr-tools">
    <div class="panel">
     <div>
      <b style="font-size:18px">Al-Qur'an</b>
      <p class="hint">Pilih nama surah dan qari. Data ayat serta audio lengkap diambil dari API.</p>
     </div>
     <div class="qr-fields">
      <div class="f">
       <label for="qr-search">Cari surah</label>
       <input id="qr-search" type="search" placeholder="Contoh: Al-Kahf" autocomplete="off">
       <select id="qr-surah" aria-label="Pilih surah" style="margin-top:8px"></select>
      </div>
      <div class="f">
       <label for="qr-qari">Pilih qari / pengisi suara</label>
       <select id="qr-qari"></select>
       <p class="qr-note" style="margin-top:7px">Pilihan qari mengikuti daftar audio yang dikirim API.</p>
      </div>
     </div>
     <div class="row"><button class="btn" id="qr-load" type="button">Baca Surat & Putar</button><button class="btn alt" id="qr-clear" type="button">Bersihkan</button></div>
     <div id="qr-message" class="hint" role="status" aria-live="polite">Pilih surah dan qari, lalu tekan “Baca Surat & Putar”.</div>
    </div>
    <div id="qr-result" class="hide">
     <div id="qr-summary" class="qr-summary"></div>
     <div class="qr-player">
      <div class="qr-player-top"><b id="qr-player-title">Audio murottal</b><small id="qr-player-qari"></small></div>
      <audio id="qr-audio" class="qr-audio" controls preload="none"></audio>
      <p id="qr-audio-note" class="qr-status">Audio akan dicoba diputar otomatis setelah surat dimuat.</p>
     </div>
     <div>
      <h2 style="margin:18px 0 10px">Bacaan ayat</h2>
      <div id="qr-ayahs" class="qr-list"></div>
     </div>
    </div>
   </div>`;

  const q=id=>el.querySelector("#"+id);
  const search=q("qr-search"),surahSel=q("qr-surah"),qariSel=q("qr-qari"),loadBtn=q("qr-load"),clearBtn=q("qr-clear");
  const message=q("qr-message"),result=q("qr-result"),summary=q("qr-summary"),audio=q("qr-audio"),audioNote=q("qr-audio-note");
  let current=null;
  const defaultSurahOptions=()=>surahs.map(([n,name])=>`<option value="${n}">${n}. ${name}</option>`).join("");
  surahSel.innerHTML=defaultSurahOptions();
  qariSel.innerHTML=defaultQaris.map(([id,name])=>`<option value="${id}">${name}</option>`).join("");
  qariSel.value="05";

  const setMessage=(text,isError=false)=>{message.textContent=text;message.classList.toggle("err",!!isError)};
  const safeAudioUrl=value=>{try{const u=new URL(value);return u.protocol==="https:"?u.href:""}catch{return ""}};
  const normalizeAudioFull=raw=>{
   if(Array.isArray(raw))return raw.map(x=>({qari_id:String(x.qari_id||x.id||""),qari:String(x.qari||x.name||"Qari"),url:String(x.url||"")})).filter(x=>x.qari_id&&x.url);
   if(raw&&typeof raw==="object")return Object.entries(raw).map(([id,value])=>typeof value==="string"?({qari_id:String(id),qari:defaultQaris.find(x=>x[0]===String(id))?.[1]||("Qari "+id),url:value}):({qari_id:String(value.qari_id||id),qari:String(value.qari||value.name||("Qari "+id)),url:String(value.url||"")})).filter(x=>x.qari_id&&x.url);
   return [];
  };
  const setQariOptions=items=>{
   const before=qariSel.value;
   qariSel.innerHTML=items.map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x.qari_id)}">${esc(x.qari)}</option>`).join("");
   if(items.some(x=>x.qari_id===before))qariSel.value=before;
   else if(items.some(x=>x.qari_id==="05"))qariSel.value="05";
   else if(items.length)qariSel.value=items[0].qari_id;
  };
  const selectedAudio=()=>{
   if(!current)return null;
   const item=current.audioFull.find(x=>x.qari_id===qariSel.value);
   if(!item)return null;
   const url=safeAudioUrl(item.url);
   return url?{...item,url}:null;
  };
  const playCurrent=async()=>{
   const item=selectedAudio();
   if(!item){audioNote.textContent="Audio untuk qari ini tidak tersedia.";return}
   audio.pause();audio.src=item.url;audio.autoplay=true;audio.load();
   q("qr-player-qari").textContent=item.qari;
   audioNote.textContent="Memuat audio " + item.qari + "…";
   try{await audio.play();audioNote.textContent="Sedang diputar: "+item.qari}
   catch{audioNote.textContent="Surat sudah dimuat. Browser mungkin menahan autoplay; tekan tombol Play pada pemutar audio."}
  };
  const renderAyahs=items=>{
   if(!Array.isArray(items)||!items.length){q("qr-ayahs").innerHTML='<div class="qr-empty">Teks ayat tidak tersedia dari respons API.</div>';return}
   q("qr-ayahs").innerHTML=items.map(a=>`<article class="qr-ayah">
    <div class="qr-ayah-head"><span>AYAT ${Number(a.nomorAyat)||"—"}</span><span>۞</span></div>
    <div class="qr-ar" lang="ar" dir="rtl">${esc(String(a.teksArab||"Teks Arab tidak tersedia"))}</div>
    ${a.teksLatin?`<div class="qr-latin">${esc(String(a.teksLatin))}</div>`:""}
    ${a.teksIndonesia?`<div class="qr-translation">${esc(String(a.teksIndonesia))}</div>`:""}
   </article>`).join("");
  };
  const loadSurah=async()=>{
   const number=Number(surahSel.value);
   if(!Number.isInteger(number)||number<1||number>114){setMessage("Nomor surah harus antara 1 sampai 114.",true);return}
   loadBtn.disabled=true;loadBtn.textContent="Memuat surat…";audio.pause();audio.removeAttribute("src");audio.load();result.classList.add("hide");
   setMessage("Mengambil data surah dan daftar qari dari API…");
   try{
    const endpoint="https://api.nexadev.my.id/api/quranaudio/?surah="+number+"&surat="+number+"&mode=full";
    const response=await fetch(endpoint,{method:"GET",headers:{Accept:"application/json"}});
    const raw=await response.text();let json;
    try{json=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{throw new Error("API tidak mengembalikan JSON yang valid (HTTP "+response.status+").")}
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error("API gagal dengan HTTP "+response.status+".");
    if(json&&json.success===false)throw new Error(json.message||json.error||"API menolak permintaan.");
    const data=json&&json.data&&typeof json.data==="object"?json.data:json;
    if(!data||!Array.isArray(data.ayat))throw new Error("Data ayat tidak ditemukan dalam respons API.");
    const audioFull=normalizeAudioFull(data.audioFull);
    if(!audioFull.length)throw new Error("Daftar audio qari tidak tersedia untuk surah ini.");
    current={data,audioFull};setQariOptions(audioFull);
    const name=String(data.namaLatin||surahs.find(x=>x[0]===number)?.[1]||("Surah "+number));
    const arabicName=String(data.nama||"");
    summary.innerHTML=`<div><b>${esc(name)}</b><small>Surah ke-${Number(data.nomor)||number}${arabicName?" · "+esc(arabicName):""}</small></div><div><b>${Number(data.jumlahAyat)||data.ayat.length}</b><small>Jumlah ayat</small></div><div><b>${esc(String(data.tempatTurun||"—"))}</b><small>Tempat turun</small></div>${data.arti?`<div><b>${esc(String(data.arti))}</b><small>Arti nama</small></div>`:""}`;
    renderAyahs(data.ayat);result.classList.remove("hide");
    const option=selectedAudio();if(!option)throw new Error("URL audio qari yang dipilih tidak tersedia.");
    q("qr-player-title").textContent="Murottal — "+name;
    await playCurrent();
    setMessage("Berhasil memuat "+name+". "+data.ayat.length+" ayat ditampilkan.");
   }catch(error){
    const isNetwork=error instanceof TypeError;
    setMessage(isNetwork?"Gagal menghubungi API. Periksa koneksi atau izin CORS API untuk domain website ini.":(error&&error.message?error.message:"Terjadi kesalahan saat memuat surah."),true);
   }finally{loadBtn.disabled=false;loadBtn.textContent="Baca Surat & Putar"}
  };

  search.addEventListener("input",()=>{
   const term=search.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("id");
   const filtered=surahs.filter(([n,name])=>String(n).includes(term)||name.toLocaleLowerCase("id").includes(term));
   const currentValue=surahSel.value;
   surahSel.innerHTML=filtered.length?filtered.map(([n,name])=>`<option value="${n}">${n}. ${name}</option>`).join(""):'<option value="">Surah tidak ditemukan</option>';
   if(filtered.some(x=>String(x[0])===currentValue))surahSel.value=currentValue;
  });
  loadBtn.addEventListener("click",loadSurah);
  qariSel.addEventListener("change",()=>{if(current)playCurrent()});
  clearBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>{audio.pause();audio.removeAttribute("src");audio.load();current=null;result.classList.add("hide");search.value="";surahSel.innerHTML=defaultSurahOptions();surahSel.value="1";qariSel.innerHTML=defaultQaris.map(([id,name])=>`<option value="${id}">${name}</option>`).join("");qariSel.value="05";setMessage("Pilihan dibersihkan. Pilih surah untuk mulai lagi.")});
  audio.addEventListener("play",()=>{audioNote.textContent="Sedang diputar: "+(selectedAudio()?.qari||"qari terpilih")});
  audio.addEventListener("ended",()=>{audioNote.textContent="Murottal selesai diputar."});
  audio.addEventListener("error",()=>{if(audio.currentSrc)audioNote.textContent="Audio gagal diputar. Coba pilih qari lain atau muat ulang surah."});
  addEventListener("hashchange",()=>{if(location.hash!=="#tool-quran-reader")audio.pause()});
 }
});

   busy=v;thinking.classList.toggle("show",v);
   startBtn.disabled=v;backBtn.disabled=v||!state;
   excludeBtn.disabled=v||!state;
   answers.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b=>b.disabled=v);

  const setProgress=value=>{
   let n=Number(value);if(!Number.isFinite(n))n=0;
   n=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,n<=1?n*100:n));
   bar.style.width=n+"%";pct.textContent=Math.round(n)+"%";
  };
  const pickText=obj=>{
   if(!obj||typeof obj!=="object")return "";
   const keys=["character","guess","name","title","person","akinetor","akinator","answer"];
   for(const key of keys){
    const v=obj[key];
    if(typeof v==="string"&&v.trim())return v.trim();
    if(v&&typeof v==="object"){const nested=pickText(v);if(nested)return nested}

  };
  const request=async({answer,reset=false}={})=>{
   if(busy)return;
   setBusy(true);status.textContent=reset?"Memulai permainan baru…":"Mengirim jawaban…";
   if(reset){state="";step=0;finish.classList.remove("show");answers.style.display="none";question.textContent="Wilnstor sedang menyiapkan permainan…";setProgress(0)}
   try{
    const params=new URLSearchParams({theme,apikey:KEY});
    if(!reset&&state){params.set("state",state);if(answer!==undefined)params.set("answer",answer)}
    const res=await fetch(API+"?"+params.toString(),{method:"GET",headers:{Accept:"application/json"}});
    let data;try{data=await res.json()}catch{throw new Error("Respons API bukan JSON yang valid.")}
    if(!res.ok||data.status===false)throw new Error(data.message||data.error||("API gagal (HTTP "+res.status+")."));
    const result=data.result&&typeof data.result==="object"?data.result:data;
    if(typeof result.state==="string"&&result.state)state=result.state;
    lastResult=result;
    if(result.question&&Array.isArray(result.answers))renderQuestion(result);
    else showEnd(result);
   }catch(error){
    status.textContent=error&&error.message?error.message:"Gagal menghubungi API. Coba lagi.";
    if(!state){question.textContent="Belum bisa memulai permainan.";answers.style.display="none"}
   }finally{setBusy(false)}
  };
  const sendAnswer=answer=>request({answer});
  startBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>request({reset:true}));
  backBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>request({answer:"back"}));
  excludeBtn.addEventListener("click",()=>request({answer:"exclude"}));
  $("wn-again").addEventListener("click",()=>request({reset:true}));
  /* Sesi baru otomatis dimulai sekali ketika halaman tool dibuka. */
  request({reset:true});
 }
});


/* ============ Web to APK V2 — Izzxd API ============ */
TOOLS.push({
 id:"web2apk-v2",name:"Web to APK V2",desc:"Ubah website menjadi aplikasi Android APK.",cat:"Utilities",featured:false,
 icon:'<rect x="5" y="3" width="14" height="18" rx="2"/><path d="M9 7h6M9 17h6"/>',
 render(el){
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="panel">
   <div class="f"><label for="w2a-name">Nama aplikasi</label><input id="w2a-name" type="text" placeholder="Contoh: Xaerisoft" maxlength="80"></div>
   <div class="f"><label for="w2a-package">Package name</label><input id="w2a-package" type="text" placeholder="com.example.xaerisoft" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false"></div>
   <div class="f"><label for="w2a-url">URL website</label><input id="w2a-url" type="text" placeholder="https://example.com" inputmode="url" autocomplete="url" spellcheck="false"></div>
   <div class="row"><button class="btn" id="w2a-submit" type="button">Buat APK</button></div>
   <div id="w2a-msg" class="hint" role="status" aria-live="polite">Isi nama aplikasi, package name, dan URL website.</div>
   <div id="w2a-result"></div>
  </div>`;
  const $id=id=>el.querySelector("#"+id);
  const name=$id("w2a-name"),pkg=$id("w2a-package"),url=$id("w2a-url");
  const btn=$id("w2a-submit"),msg=$id("w2a-msg"),out=$id("w2a-result");
  const addLink=(href,label)=>{
   try{
    const u=new URL(href);
    if(!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol))return;
    const a=document.createElement("a");a.className="btn";a.href=u.href;a.target="_blank";a.rel="noopener noreferrer";a.textContent=label;out.appendChild(a);
   }catch{}
  };
  const collectLinks=(value,seen=new Set())=>{
   if(!value||typeof value!=="object"||seen.has(value))return [];
   seen.add(value);let found=[];
   for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
    if(typeof v==="string"&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(v)&&/(apk|download|url|link|file)/i.test(k))found.push({key:k,url:v});
    else if(v&&typeof v==="object")found=found.concat(collectLinks(v,seen));
   }
   return found;
  };
  const submit=async()=>{
   const appname=name.value.trim(),packageName=pkg.value.trim(),site=url.value.trim();
   if(!appname||!packageName||!site){msg.textContent="Semua kolom wajib diisi.";return}
   if(!/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(\.[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)+$/.test(packageName)){msg.textContent="Format package name tidak valid. Contoh: com.example.app";return}
   let parsed;try{parsed=new URL(site)}catch{msg.textContent="URL website tidak valid.";return}
   if(!/^https?:$/.test(parsed.protocol)){msg.textContent="URL harus menggunakan http:// atau https://.";return}
   btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent="Memproses...";msg.textContent="Mengirim permintaan ke API Web to APK V2...";out.replaceChildren();
   try{
    const params=new URLSearchParams({appname,package:packageName,url:parsed.href,apikey:"izzxd-86cpjw6"});
    const response=await fetch("https://api.izzxd.my.id/api/tools/web2apk-v2?"+params.toString());
    const raw=await response.text();let data;
    try{data=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{throw new Error("Respons API bukan JSON yang valid.")};
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error("API mengembalikan HTTP "+response.status);
    if(data&&((data.status===false)||(data.success===false)||(data.ok===false)))throw new Error(data.message||data.msg||"API gagal memproses permintaan.");
    const pre=document.createElement("pre");pre.className="out";pre.style.whiteSpace="pre-wrap";pre.textContent=JSON.stringify(data,null,2);out.appendChild(pre);
    const links=collectLinks(data);
    const unique=[...new Map(links.map(x=>[x.url,x])).values()];
    if(unique.length){
     const heading=document.createElement("p");heading.className="hint";heading.style.marginTop="10px";heading.textContent="Tautan hasil:";out.appendChild(heading);
     unique.forEach(x=>addLink(x.url,"Buka "+x.key));
    }else{
     const note=document.createElement("p");note.className="hint";note.style.marginTop="10px";note.textContent="Respons API diterima. Periksa data di atas untuk melihat hasil atau status proses.";out.appendChild(note);
    }
    msg.textContent="Permintaan selesai.";
   }catch(err){msg.textContent=err&&err.message?err.message:"Gagal menghubungi API. Periksa koneksi atau dukungan CORS."}
   finally{btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent="Buat APK"}
  };
  btn.addEventListener("click",submit);
  [name,pkg,url].forEach(input=>input.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter")submit()}));
 }
});

TOOLS.push({
 id:"wa-pairing",name:"Pairing Code WhatsApp",desc:"Dapatkan kode pairing WhatsApp tanpa scan QR.",cat:"Utilities",featured:false,
 icon:'<path d="M21 11.5a8.5 8.5 0 01-12.3 7.6L4 20l1-4.6A8.5 8.5 0 1121 11.5z"/><path d="M8 10.5h8M8 13.5h5"/>',
 render(el){
  el.innerHTML=`<style>
   .wap-card{display:grid;gap:14px;padding:18px;border:2px solid var(--bd);border-radius:20px;background:var(--card);box-shadow:4px 4px 0 var(--blue)}

  </style><div class="wap-card">

   if(o==null||d>6||typeof o!=="object"||seen.has(o))return "";
   seen.add(o);
   for(const k in o){
    const v=o[k];
    if(typeof v==="string"&&/^[A-Z0-9]{4}(-?[A-Z0-9]{4})?$/.test(v.trim())&&/code|pair|kode|otp/i.test(k))return v.trim();
   }
   for(const k in o){const v=o[k];if(v&&typeof v==="object"){const f=findCode(v,d+1,seen);if(f)return f}}
   return "";
  };
  const submit=async()=>{
   const n=nomor.value.replace(/[^\d]/g,"");
   if(!n||n.length<8){msg.textContent="Masukkan nomor WhatsApp yang valid (pakai kode negara, tanpa + atau 0 di depan).";return}
   btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent="Memproses...";msg.textContent="Sedang memproses permintaan...";out.replaceChildren();out.classList.remove("show");
   try{
    const params=new URLSearchParams({nomor:n,apikey:"izzxd-86cpjw6"});
    const response=await fetch("https://api.izzxd.my.id/api/tools/sp4motp?"+params.toString());
    const raw=await response.text();let data;
    try{data=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{throw new Error("Respons API bukan JSON yang valid.")}
    if(!response.ok)throw new Error(data&&(data.message||data.msg)||("API mengembalikan HTTP "+response.status));

    }else{

/* ============ NASA Name Generator ============
   Mengubah nama menjadi huruf dari citra satelit Landsat (sumber data: nasanamegenerator.com).
   Logika scraper (scrape-NasaNameGenerator.js) sudah dipindah inline: halaman sumber diambil dan
   gambar gabungan disusun langsung di browser (canvas). Itu hanya jalan bila situs sumber mengizinkan
   permintaan lintas-origin (CORS). Bila diblokir, tool otomatis beralih ke backend Node.js (folder backend/)
   jika NASA_API_BASE diisi, contoh: "https://nasa-name-xxxxx.a.run.app" (tanpa garis miring di akhir). */
const NASA_API_BASE="";
/* NASA_PROXY: URL Cloudflare Worker perantara (lihat worker/cloudflare-worker.js), tanpa garis miring di akhir,
   contoh: "https://nasa-name.namakamu.workers.dev". Bila diisi, tool memakainya lebih dulu (cara paling praktis
   kalau situs sumber memblokir akses langsung dari browser). */
const NASA_PROXY="https://icy-dust-e435.ujicobakun299.workers.dev";
const nnPx=()=>NASA_PROXY.replace(/\/+$/,"");
const NN_BASE="https://nasanamegenerator.com";
let nnEngineCache=null,nnEngineAt=0;
const nnFetch=(u,o,ms)=>{let t;return Promise.race([fetch(u,o),new Promise((_,no)=>{t=setTimeout(()=>no(Object.assign(new Error("waktu habis"),{name:"AbortError"})),ms)})]).finally(()=>clearTimeout(t))};
const nnParse=html=>{
 const s=html.match(/const letterDataSerialized = "((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)";/);
 if(!s)throw new Error("data huruf tidak ditemukan di halaman sumber");
 const data=JSON.parse(JSON.parse('"'+s[1]+'"'));
 const rawBase=(html.match(/const imagesBasePath = "([^"]+)"/)||[])[1]||"/images/letters";
 const base=rawBase.startsWith("http")?rawBase.replace(/\/$/,""):NN_BASE+(rawBase.startsWith("/")?"":"/")+rawBase.replace(/\/$/,"");
 let raw={};
 const pb=html.match(/function createLetterArrays\(\)\s*\{\s*return\s*\{([\s\S]*?)\};\s*\}/);
 if(pb){try{raw=JSON.parse("{"+pb[1].replace(/([A-Z])\s*:/g,'"$1":')+"}")}catch{raw={}}}
 const pools={};
 for(const l of Object.keys(data)){const d=raw[l.toUpperCase()];pools[l]=Array.isArray(d)?d.filter(i=>Number.isInteger(i)&&data[l][i]):data[l].map((_,i)=>i)}
 return{data,pools,base};
};
const nnLoad=async()=>{
 if(nnEngineCache&&Date.now()-nnEngineAt<36e5)return nnEngineCache;
 const r=await nnFetch(NASA_PROXY?nnPx()+"/page":NN_BASE+"/",undefined,20000);
 if(!r.ok){let m="";try{const e=await r.json();m=e&&e.error||""}catch{}throw new Error(m||"HTTP "+r.status)}
 nnEngineCache=nnParse(await r.text());nnEngineAt=Date.now();return nnEngineCache;
};
const nnCleanLink=u=>!u||u==="https://none.com"?null:u;
const nnMaps=m=>m.maps&&m.maps!=="https://maps.google.com"?m.maps:m.coordinates?"https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query="+encodeURIComponent(m.coordinates):null;
const nnFrame=async(tiles,blobs)=>{
 const imgs=[];for(const t of tiles)imgs.push(await createImageBitmap(blobs.get(t.tile)));
 const gaps=[];let prev=0;for(const t of tiles){gaps.push(Math.max(0,t.position-prev-1));prev=t.position}
 const W=imgs.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.width,0)+25*(imgs.length-1)+gaps.slice(1).reduce((s,c)=>s+c*60,0);
 const H=Math.max(...imgs.map(i=>i.height));
 if(W*H>3e7)throw new Error("gambar terlalu besar");
 const c=document.createElement("canvas");c.width=W;c.height=H;
 const x=c.getContext("2d");x.fillStyle="#fff";x.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 let ox=0;imgs.forEach((im,i)=>{if(i>0)ox+=25+gaps[i]*60;x.drawImage(im,ox,0);ox+=im.width;if(im.close)im.close()});
 let url=c.toDataURL("image/webp",.9);if(!/^data:image\/webp/.test(url))url=c.toDataURL("image/png");
 return{width:W,height:H,image:url};
};
const nnInline=async name=>{
 const eng=await nnLoad(),used=new Set(),tiles=[];let pos=0;
 for(const ch of name){
  pos+=1;if(ch===" ")continue;
  const l=ch.toLowerCase(),pool=eng.pools[l]||[];if(!pool.length)continue;
  let cand=pool.filter(i=>!used.has(l+"_"+i));if(!cand.length)cand=pool;
  const idx=cand[rand(cand.length)];used.add(l+"_"+idx);
  const m=(eng.data[l]&&eng.data[l][idx])||{};
  tiles.push({position:pos,char:ch.toUpperCase(),tile:l+"_"+idx,location:m.location||null,coordinates:m.coordinates||null,link:nnCleanLink(m.link),maps:nnMaps(m),url:eng.base+"/letter_"+l+"_"+idx+".webp",image:null});
 }
 if(!tiles.length)throw Object.assign(new Error("Tidak ada ubin Landsat untuk nama ini."),{shown:true});
 const blobs=new Map(),failed=[];
 await Promise.all(tiles.map(async t=>{
  t.image=t.url;
  try{if(!/^https:\/\//.test(t.url))throw 0;const r=await nnFetch(NASA_PROXY?nnPx()+"/img?u="+encodeURIComponent(t.url):t.url,undefined,20000);if(!r.ok)throw 0;blobs.set(t.tile,await r.blob())}catch{failed.push(t.tile)}
 }));
 let frame=null,frameError=null;
 if(failed.length)frameError="beberapa gambar ubin tidak bisa diunduh dari browser";
 else try{frame=await nnFrame(tiles,blobs)}catch(e){frameError=e&&e.message||"gagal menyusun gambar"}
 const seq=tiles.map(t=>t.tile.split("_")[1]).join("");
 return{meta:{status:200,response:"OK",name,totalTiles:tiles.length,shareUrl:NN_BASE+"/"+encodeURIComponent(name)+"?v="+seq,...(failed.length?{failedImages:failed}:{}),...(frameError?{frameError}:{})},data:tiles,frame};
};
TOOLS.push({
 id:"nasa-name",name:"NASA Name Generator",desc:"Ubah namamu jadi huruf dari citra satelit Landsat NASA.",cat:"Fun Tools",featured:false,
 icon:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9"/><path d="M3 12h18"/><path d="M12 3c3 3 3 15 0 18"/><path d="M12 3c-3 3-3 15 0 18"/>',
 render(el){
  el.innerHTML=`<style>
.nn-out{display:grid;gap:14px}
.nn-frame{overflow-x:auto;border:2px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;background:#fff;padding:8px}
.nn-frame img{display:block;height:auto;width:max(100%,calc(var(--n,1)*72px))}
.nn-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(150px,1fr));gap:10px}
.nn-card{display:grid;gap:6px;align-content:start;border:1.5px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;background:var(--card);padding:8px;min-width:0}
.nn-card img{display:block;width:100%;aspect-ratio:1;object-fit:cover;border-radius:10px;background:var(--light)}
.nn-card b{font-size:15px}
.nn-card p{font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.35;color:var(--mute);overflow-wrap:anywhere}
.nn-links{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-size:13px;font-weight:700}
.nn-links a{color:var(--ink);text-decoration:underline}
</style>
<div class="panel">
 <div class="f"><label for="nn-name">Nama kamu (huruf A–Z dan spasi, maks. 18 karakter)</label>
  <input id="nn-name" type="text" maxlength="18" placeholder="Contoh: Wildev" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false"></div>
 <div class="row">
  <button class="btn" id="nn-go" type="button">Buat</button>
  <button class="btn alt hide" id="nn-again" type="button">Acak ulang</button>
 </div>
 <div id="nn-msg" class="hint" role="status" aria-live="polite">Masukkan nama lalu tekan Buat.</div>
 <div id="nn-out" class="nn-out"></div>
</div>`;

  const input=el.querySelector("#nn-name"),go=el.querySelector("#nn-go"),again=el.querySelector("#nn-again"),
   msg=el.querySelector("#nn-msg"),out=el.querySelector("#nn-out");
  let busy=false,lastName="";

  const say=(t,bad)=>{msg.textContent=t;msg.className=bad?"err":"hint"};
  const setBusy=v=>{busy=v;go.disabled=v;again.disabled=v;go.textContent=v?"Memproses…":"Buat"};
  const safeUrl=(u,re)=>typeof u==="string"&&re.test(u)?u:"";
  const mk=(tag,attrs,kids)=>{
   const n=document.createElement(tag);
   for(const k in attrs||{})n.setAttribute(k,attrs[k]);
   for(const c of [].concat(kids||[]))n.append(c);
   return n;
  };
  const link=(href,label)=>mk("a",{href,target:"_blank",rel:"noopener noreferrer"},label);

  const errText=(status,j)=>{
   const m=j&&j.meta&&j.meta.error?String(j.meta.error).replace(/^ERROR \d+ [^:]*:\s*/,""):"";
   if(status===429)return "Terlalu banyak permintaan. Tunggu sebentar lalu coba lagi.";
   if(status===400)return m||"Nama tidak valid. Gunakan huruf A–Z dan spasi, maksimal 18 karakter.";
   if(status===404)return m||"Tidak ada ubin Landsat untuk nama ini.";
   if(status===503)return "Server sedang sibuk. Coba lagi sebentar lagi.";
   return m||"Gagal membuat gambar (kode "+status+"). Situs sumber mungkin sedang bermasalah.";
  };

  const show=(j)=>{
   out.replaceChildren();
   const meta=j.meta,tiles=Array.isArray(j.data)?j.data:[];
   const frameImg=j.frame&&safeUrl(j.frame.image,/^data:image\/(?:webp|png);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/),ext=/^data:image\/png/.test(frameImg||"")?"png":"webp";
   if(frameImg){
    const img=mk("img",{src:frameImg,alt:"Nama "+meta.name+" dalam huruf citra satelit Landsat",width:j.frame.width||"",height:j.frame.height||""});
    const box=mk("div",{class:"nn-frame"},img);
    box.style.setProperty("--n",String(tiles.length||1));
    out.append(box);
    const row=mk("div",{class:"row"});
    const dl=mk("a",{class:"btn",href:frameImg,download:"nasa-name-"+meta.name.replace(/\s+/g,"-").toLowerCase()+"."+ext},"Unduh gambar ("+ext.toUpperCase()+")");
    row.append(dl);
    const share=safeUrl(meta.shareUrl,/^https:\/\//);
    if(share)row.append(Object.assign(link(share,"Buka di situs sumber"),{className:"btn alt"}));
    out.append(row);
   }else if(meta.frameError){
    out.append(mk("p",{class:"hint"},"Gambar gabungan tidak tersedia: "+meta.frameError));
   }
   if(meta.failedImages&&meta.failedImages.length)out.append(mk("p",{class:"hint"},meta.failedImages.length+" gambar ubin gagal diunduh server; ubin tetap dimuat langsung dari sumber."));
   const grid=mk("div",{class:"nn-grid"});
   for(const t of tiles){
    const card=mk("div",{class:"nn-card"});
    const src=safeUrl(t.image||t.url,/^https:\/\//);
    if(src)card.append(mk("img",{src,alt:"Huruf "+t.char,loading:"lazy",referrerpolicy:"no-referrer"}));
    card.append(mk("b",{},"Huruf "+t.char));
    card.append(mk("p",{},[t.location||"Lokasi tidak diketahui",t.coordinates?" · "+t.coordinates:""].join("")));
    const links=mk("div",{class:"nn-links"});
    const maps=safeUrl(t.maps,/^https?:\/\//),info=safeUrl(t.link,/^https?:\/\//);
    if(maps)links.append(link(maps,"Peta"));
    if(info)links.append(link(info,"Info"));
    if(links.childNodes.length)card.append(links);
    grid.append(card);
   }
   out.append(grid);
  };

  const run=async()=>{
   if(busy)return;
   const name=input.value.trim();
   if(!name){say("Nama masih kosong.",true);return}
   if(name.length>18){say("Nama maksimal 18 karakter.",true);return}
   if(!/^[A-Za-z ]+$/.test(name)||!/[A-Za-z]/.test(name)){say("Gunakan hanya huruf A–Z dan spasi.",true);return}
   setBusy(true);say("Mengambil ubin Landsat langsung dari browser…");
   let j=null,via="",why="";
   try{j=await nnInline(name);via=NASA_PROXY?"lewat Worker":"langsung dari browser"}
   catch(e){if(e&&e.shown){out.replaceChildren();say(e.message,true);setBusy(false);return}why=e&&e.name==="AbortError"?"waktu habis":(e&&e.message)||"gagal";console.warn("NASA inline gagal:",e)}
   try{
    if(!j){
     if(!NASA_API_BASE)throw Object.assign(new Error(NASA_PROXY?(/^data huruf|JSON/.test(why)?"Worker dan situs sumber berjalan, tetapi data hurufnya tidak terbaca ("+why+"). Struktur halaman sumber kemungkinan berbeda dari yang diharapkan scraper.":/^Situs sumber|^Tidak bisa menjangkau situs sumber/.test(why)?"Worker berjalan, tetapi "+why.charAt(0).toLowerCase()+why.slice(1)+".":"Perantara (Worker) gagal: "+why+". Periksa NASA_PROXY dan ALLOWED_ORIGINS di Worker."):"Browser tidak bisa menjangkau nasanamegenerator.com ("+why+"). Kemungkinan diblokir CORS atau situs sumber sedang tidak bisa diakses. Isi NASA_PROXY dengan URL Cloudflare Worker (lihat worker/cloudflare-worker.js)."),{shown:true});
     say("Browser tidak bisa mengakses sumber, mencoba lewat backend…");
     const r=await nnFetch(NASA_API_BASE.replace(/\/+$/,"")+"/api/nasa-name?name="+encodeURIComponent(name),{headers:{accept:"application/json"}},60000);
     let jj=null;try{jj=await r.json()}catch{}
     if(!r.ok||!jj||!jj.meta||jj.meta.status!==200)throw Object.assign(new Error(errText(r.status,jj)),{shown:true});
     j=jj;via="lewat backend";
    }
    say("Menyusun tampilan…");
    show(j);lastName=name;again.classList.remove("hide");
    say("Selesai ("+via+"): "+j.meta.totalTiles+" ubin untuk \""+j.meta.name+"\".");
   }catch(e){
    out.replaceChildren();
    say(e&&e.shown?e.message:e&&e.name==="AbortError"?"Server terlalu lama merespons (lebih dari 60 detik). Coba lagi.":"Tidak bisa menghubungi server backend. Periksa URL backend, koneksi internet, dan pengaturan CORS (ALLOWED_ORIGINS).",true);
   }finally{setBusy(false)}
  };

  go.addEventListener("click",run);
  again.addEventListener("click",()=>{if(lastName)input.value=lastName;run()});
  input.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter")run()});
 }
});


/* ============ TikTok Downloader ============
   Sumber data dikelola lewat TT_SOURCES. Setiap sumber punya endpoint, params(url) dan kind ("mp4").
   Tambah MP3 nanti: cukup tambahkan satu entri ber-kind "mp3" (endpoint + params) ke TT_SOURCES;
   hasilnya otomatis tampil sebagai grup unduhan sendiri. Sampai ada, tidak ada tombol MP3 yang ditampilkan. */
(function(){
const TT_HOSTS=/^(?:www\.|m\.|vm\.|vt\.|t\.|lite\.)?tiktok\.com$/i;
const TT_SOURCES={
 mp4:{kind:"mp4",title:"Video MP4",ext:"mp4",endpoint:"https://api.izzxd.my.id/api/downloader/tiktok",
  params(url){return{url,apikey:"izzxd-86cpjw6"}}}
 /* mp3:{kind:"mp3",title:"Audio MP3",ext:"mp3",endpoint:"…",params(url){return{url,apikey:"…"}}} */
};

/* ---- URL ---- */
function ttParse(raw){
 let s=String(raw||"").trim();
 if(!s)return{err:"Tempel link video TikTok terlebih dahulu."};
 if(/\s/.test(s)){                              // teks share: ambil token yang berisi link TikTok
  const tok=s.split(/\s+/).filter(x=>/tiktok\.com\//i.test(x));
  if(tok.length!==1)return{err:"Link tidak valid. Tempel satu link TikTok saja."};
  s=tok[0].replace(/^["'<(]+|["'>).,]+$/g,"");
 }
 if(!/^https?:\/\//i.test(s))s="https://"+s;
 let u;try{u=new URL(s)}catch{return{err:"Format link tidak valid."}}
 if(u.protocol!=="https:"&&u.protocol!=="http:")return{err:"Link harus diawali http atau https."};
 if(!TT_HOSTS.test(u.hostname))return{err:"Link bukan dari TikTok. Gunakan link dari tiktok.com, vm.tiktok.com, atau vt.tiktok.com."};
 if(u.pathname.length<3)return{err:"Link belum mengarah ke video TikTok tertentu."};
 u.protocol="https:";
 return{url:u.toString()};
}

/* ---- Pembaca respons (berdasarkan field yang benar-benar ada) ---- */
const isUrl=v=>typeof v==="string"&&/^https?:\/\/[^\s]+$/i.test(v.trim());
function walk(o,path,out,d){
 if(o==null||d>7)return;
 if(typeof o==="string"){if(isUrl(o))out.push({path:path.join(".").toLowerCase(),url:o.trim()});return}
 if(Array.isArray(o)){o.forEach((x,i)=>walk(x,path.concat(String(i)),out,d+1));return}
 if(typeof o==="object")for(const k in o)walk(o[k],path.concat(k),out,d+1);
}
function findKey(o,keys,test){
 const q=[o];let n=0;
 while(q.length&&n++<400){
  const c=q.shift();if(c==null||typeof c!=="object")continue;
  for(const k of keys){
   const hit=Object.keys(c).find(x=>x.toLowerCase()===k);
   if(hit!==undefined){const v=c[hit];if(!test||test(v))return v}
  }
  for(const k in c)if(c[k]&&typeof c[k]==="object")q.push(c[k]);
 }
 return undefined;
}
const asText=v=>typeof v==="string"&&v.trim()?v.trim():(typeof v==="number"?String(v):"");
function ttMeta(j){
 const m={};
 m.title=asText(findKey(j,["title","desc","description","caption","text"],v=>asText(v)));
 const a=findKey(j,["author","user","owner","creator","nickname","username","unique_id"],v=>v&&(typeof v==="object"||asText(v)));
 if(a&&typeof a==="object")m.author=asText(a.nickname)||asText(a.unique_id)||asText(a.username)||asText(a.name);
 else m.author=asText(a);
 const c=findKey(j,["cover","origin_cover","thumbnail","thumb","thumbnail_url","poster","image"],v=>isUrl(v)||(Array.isArray(v)&&isUrl(v[0])));
 m.cover=Array.isArray(c)?c[0]:(isUrl(c)?c:"");
 const dur=findKey(j,["duration"],v=>typeof v==="number"||(typeof v==="string"&&/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(v)));
 m.duration=dur!==undefined?Number(dur):null;
 const st=[["views","Ditonton",["play_count","playcount","views","view_count"]],["likes","Suka",["digg_count","diggcount","likes","like_count"]],["comments","Komentar",["comment_count","commentcount","comments"]],["shares","Dibagikan",["share_count","sharecount","shares"]]];
 m.stats=[];
 st.forEach(([,l,ks])=>{const v=findKey(j,ks,x=>(typeof x==="number")||(typeof x==="string"&&/^\d+$/.test(x)));if(v!==undefined)m.stats.push([l,Number(v)])});
 return m;
}
function ttOptions(j,src,inputUrl){
 const all=[];walk(j,[],all,0);
 const seen=new Set(),opts=[];
 const skip=/(cover|thumb|avatar|image|img|poster|music|audio|mp3|sound|profile|progress|icon)/;
 const mediaExt=/\.(mp4|mov|webm)(\?|#|$)/i;
 all.forEach(({path,url})=>{
  if(seen.has(url)||url===inputUrl)return;
  let host="";try{host=new URL(url).hostname}catch{return}
  if(TT_HOSTS.test(host))return;               // halaman web TikTok, bukan file
  const isMedia=mediaExt.test(url);
  if(!isMedia&&skip.test(path))return;
  if(!isMedia&&!/(video|play|download|nowm|hd|mp4|wm|dl|url|link|src|media)/.test(path))return;
  seen.add(url);
  let label="Video MP4",rank=5;
  if(/(nowm|no_wm|nowatermark|no_watermark|without|tanpa)/.test(path)){label="Tanpa watermark";rank=1}
  else if(/(^|[^a-z])hd([^a-z]|$)/.test(path)){label="Kualitas HD";rank=2}
  else if(/(^|[^a-z])wm([^a-z]|$)|watermark/.test(path)){label="Dengan watermark";rank=9}
  opts.push({label,url,rank});
 });
 opts.sort((a,b)=>a.rank-b.rank);
 const n={};opts.forEach(o=>{n[o.label]=(n[o.label]||0)+1;if(n[o.label]>1)o.label+=" ("+n[o.label]+")"});
 return opts;
}
function ttFailure(j){
 if(!j||typeof j!=="object")return"Respons dari server tidak dikenali.";
 const bad=j.status===false||j.success===false||j.ok===false||j.error===true||(typeof j.status==="number"&&j.status>=400)||(typeof j.code==="number"&&j.code>=400)||(typeof j.status==="string"&&/^(error|fail|failed)$/i.test(j.status))||(typeof j.error==="string"&&j.error);
 if(!bad)return"";
 let msg=asText(j.message)||asText(j.msg)||asText(j.error)||asText(j.detail)||asText(j.pesan)||asText(j.info);
 if(!msg)for(const k in j){if(typeof j[k]==="string"&&j[k].trim()&&!/^(status|success|ok|code)$/i.test(k)){msg=j[k].trim();break}}
 if(/\bapi(key|-key)?\b/i.test(msg))msg="";
 return msg||"Layanan sedang bermasalah. Coba lagi beberapa saat lagi.";
}
async function ttCall(src,url,signal){
 const res=await fetch(src.endpoint+"?"+new URLSearchParams(src.params(url)),{signal});
 let j=null,txt="";
 try{txt=await res.text();j=JSON.parse(txt)}catch{}
 if(!res.ok){const e=new Error(ttFailure(j)||("Server mengembalikan status "+res.status+"."));e.shown=true;throw e}
 if(j===null){const e=new Error("Respons server bukan JSON yang valid.");e.shown=true;throw e}
 const f=ttFailure(j);if(f){const e=new Error(f);e.shown=true;throw e}
 return{json:j,meta:ttMeta(j),options:ttOptions(j,src,url)};
}
const fmtN=n=>n>=1e6?(n/1e6).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/,"")+" jt":n>=1e3?(n/1e3).toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/,"")+" rb":String(n);
const fmtD=s=>{s=Math.round(s);if(s>86400)s=Math.round(s/1000);return Math.floor(s/60)+":"+String(s%60).padStart(2,"0")};
const DL='<path d="M12 4v10"/><path d="M8 10l4 4 4-4"/><path d="M5 19h14"/>';
const PASTE='<rect x="8" y="3" width="8" height="4" rx="1"/><path d="M16 5h2a2 2 0 012 2v12a2 2 0 01-2 2H6a2 2 0 01-2-2V7a2 2 0 012-2h2"/>';

TOOLS.push({
 id:"tiktok-downloader",name:"TikTok Downloader",desc:"Unduh video TikTok dalam format MP4 hanya dengan menempel link.",cat:"downloader",featured:false,
 icon:'<path d="M12 4v10"/><path d="M8 10l4 4 4-4"/><path d="M5 19h14"/>',
 render(el){
  el.innerHTML=`<style>
.tt-in{display:flex;gap:8px}.tt-in input{flex:1;min-width:0}
.tt-in .btn.alt{padding:0 14px;justify-content:center}
.tt-go{width:100%;justify-content:center;gap:8px}
.tt-go[disabled]{opacity:.65;cursor:progress}

@keyframes ttspin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.tt-sp{animation-duration:2s}}
.tt-load{display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:center;border:1px dashed var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:14px;color:var(--mute);font-size:14px}

.tt-res{display:grid;gap:14px}
.tt-top{display:flex;gap:14px;align-items:flex-start}

.tt-th img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;display:block}
.tt-inf{min-width:0;display:grid;gap:8px}
.tt-ti{font-weight:700;line-height:1.35;overflow-wrap:anywhere;display:-webkit-box;-webkit-line-clamp:4;-webkit-box-orient:vertical;overflow:hidden}

.tt-op .btn{gap:6px;padding:10px 14px;flex:none}
.tt-op .btn svg{width:18px;height:18px}
.tt-dbg{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--mute)}
.tt-dbg summary{cursor:pointer;min-height:32px;display:flex;align-items:center}
.tt-dbg pre{max-height:220px;overflow:auto;background:var(--bg);border-radius:10px;padding:10px;font-size:12px;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all;margin-top:6px}
@media(min-width:640px){.tt-th{width:160px}.tt-top{gap:18px}.tt-ol{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}}

</style>
<div class="panel">
 <div class="f">
  <label for="tt-url">Link video TikTok</label>
  <div class="tt-in">
   <input id="tt-url" type="text" inputmode="url" placeholder="https://www.tiktok.com/@akun/video/…" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false">
   <button class="btn alt" id="tt-paste" type="button" aria-label="Tempel dari clipboard" title="Tempel">${ico(PASTE)}</button>
  </div>

 <button class="btn tt-go" id="tt-go" type="button">${ico(DL)}<span>Download</span></button>

 <div id="tt-out"></div>
</div>`;
  const $$=s=>el.querySelector(s);
  const input=$$("#tt-url"),go=$$("#tt-go"),msg=$$("#tt-msg"),out=$$("#tt-out");
  let ctl=null,seq=0;
  const say=(t,bad)=>{msg.className=bad?"err":"hint";msg.textContent=t};
  const busy=b=>{go.disabled=b;go.querySelector("span").textContent=b?"Memproses…":"Download";input.readOnly=b};

  $$("#tt-paste").addEventListener("click",async()=>{
   try{const t=await navigator.clipboard.readText();if(!t)return toast("Clipboard kosong");input.value=t.trim();input.focus()}
   catch{toast("Tidak bisa membaca clipboard, tempel manual")}
  });
  input.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.key==="Enter")run()});
  go.addEventListener("click",run);

  async function save(opt,btn,ext){
   const lab=btn.querySelector("span"),old=lab.textContent;
   btn.disabled=true;lab.textContent="Mengunduh…";
   try{
    const r=await fetch(opt.url);if(!r.ok)throw 0;
    const b=await r.blob();if(!b.size)throw 0;
    const a=document.createElement("a");a.href=URL.createObjectURL(b);a.download="xaerisoft-tiktok-"+Date.now()+"."+ext;
    document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),4000);
    toast("Unduhan dimulai");
   }catch{
    /* Server file tidak mengizinkan unduhan langsung dari browser: buka di tab baru */
    window.open(opt.url,"_blank","noopener,noreferrer");
    toast("Dibuka di tab baru. Tahan/tekan lama video untuk menyimpan.");
   }finally{btn.disabled=false;lab.textContent=old}
  }

  function show(url,groups,first){
   const {meta}=first;
   const box=document.createElement("div");box.className="tt-res enter";
   const top=document.createElement("div");top.className="tt-top";
   const th=document.createElement("div");th.className="tt-th";
   if(meta.cover){
    const im=new Image();im.alt="Thumbnail video";im.referrerPolicy="no-referrer";im.loading="lazy";
    im.onerror=()=>{th.innerHTML=ico(DL)};im.src=meta.cover;th.appendChild(im);
   }else th.innerHTML=ico(DL);
   const inf=document.createElement("div");inf.className="tt-inf";
   if(meta.title){const t=document.createElement("div");t.className="tt-ti";t.textContent=meta.title;inf.appendChild(t)}
   if(meta.author){const a=document.createElement("div");a.className="tt-au";a.textContent="Oleh "+meta.author;inf.appendChild(a)}
   const chips=[];
   if(meta.duration!==null&&isFinite(meta.duration))chips.push(["Durasi",fmtD(meta.duration)]);
   meta.stats.forEach(s=>chips.push([s[0],fmtN(s[1])]));
   if(chips.length){const st=document.createElement("div");st.className="tt-st";chips.forEach(([l,v])=>{const s=document.createElement("span");s.append(l+" ");const b=document.createElement("b");b.textContent=v;s.appendChild(b);st.appendChild(s)});inf.appendChild(st)}
   if(!inf.children.length){const t=document.createElement("div");t.className="tt-ti";t.textContent="Video TikTok";inf.appendChild(t)}
   top.append(th,inf);box.appendChild(top);
   groups.forEach(g=>{
    const h=document.createElement("div");h.className="tt-gh";h.textContent="Pilihan unduhan · "+g.src.title;box.appendChild(h);
    const ol=document.createElement("ul");ol.className="tt-ol";
    g.options.forEach(o=>{
     const li=document.createElement("li");li.className="tt-op";
     const d=document.createElement("div");const b=document.createElement("b");b.textContent=o.label;const s=document.createElement("small");s.textContent=g.src.ext.toUpperCase();d.append(b,s);
     const btn=document.createElement("button");btn.type="button";btn.className="btn";btn.innerHTML=ico(DL)+"<span>Unduh</span>";
     btn.setAttribute("aria-label","Unduh "+o.label);
     btn.addEventListener("click",()=>save(o,btn,g.src.ext));
     li.append(d,btn);ol.appendChild(li);
    });
    box.appendChild(ol);
   });
   out.replaceChildren(box);
  }

  async function run(){
   if(go.disabled)return;
   const p=ttParse(input.value);
   out.replaceChildren();
   if(p.err){say(p.err,true);input.focus();return}
   input.value=p.url;
   if(ctl)ctl.abort();ctl=new AbortController();
   const my=++seq,timer=setTimeout(()=>ctl.abort("timeout"),30000),signal=ctl.signal;
   busy(true);say("Mengambil data video…");
   out.innerHTML='<div class="tt-load" role="status"><span class="tt-sp" aria-hidden="true"></span><span>Sedang memproses link, mohon tunggu sebentar…</span></div>';
   try{
    const entries=Object.values(TT_SOURCES);
    const rs=await Promise.allSettled(entries.map(s=>ttCall(s,p.url,signal)));
    if(my!==seq)return;
    const groups=[];let firstErr=null;
    rs.forEach((r,i)=>{
     if(r.status==="fulfilled"){if(r.value.options.length)groups.push({src:entries[i],options:r.value.options,meta:r.value.meta,json:r.value.json});else if(!firstErr){firstErr=Object.assign(new Error("Server merespons, tetapi tidak ada tautan unduhan yang ditemukan. Pastikan video bersifat publik."),{shown:true,json:r.value.json})}}
     else if(!firstErr)firstErr=r.reason;
    });
    if(!groups.length)throw firstErr||new Error("Gagal memproses video.");
    show(p.url,groups,groups[0]);
    say("Video ditemukan. Pilih salah satu unduhan di bawah.");
   }catch(e){
    if(my!==seq)return;
    out.replaceChildren();
    let t;
    if(e&&e.shown)t=e.message;
    else if(signal.aborted||(e&&e.name==="AbortError"))t="Server terlalu lama merespons. Coba lagi beberapa saat lagi.";
    else if(e instanceof TypeError)t="Tidak dapat terhubung ke server. Periksa koneksi internet kamu lalu coba lagi.";
    else t="Terjadi kesalahan tak terduga. Coba lagi.";
    say(t,true);
    console.error("TikTok Downloader:",e);
   }finally{clearTimeout(timer);if(my===seq)busy(false)}
  }
 }
});
})();


/* ============ Jadi Hitam — XYurei API ============ */

 icon:'<path d="M12 3v18"/><path d="M5 7h14"/><path d="M7 7l-3 6h6L7 7z"/><path d="M17 7l-3 6h6l-3-6z"/><path d="M8 21h8"/>',
 render(el){
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="panel">
   <div class="f">
    <label for="jh-url">URL gambar (opsional)</label>
    <input id="jh-url" type="text" placeholder="https://contoh.com/gambar.jpg" autocomplete="url" spellcheck="false">
   </div>
   <div class="hint" style="text-align:center">— atau unggah gambar dari perangkat —</div>
   <div class="f">
    <label for="jh-file">Pilih gambar</label>
    <input id="jh-file" type="file" accept="image/*">
   </div>
   <div class="hint">Jika memilih file, gambar akan diunggah sementara untuk mendapatkan URL sebelum diproses.</div>

   <div id="jh-msg" class="hint" role="status" aria-live="polite">Masukkan URL atau pilih gambar.</div>
   <div id="jh-out"></div>
  </div>`;
  const $=s=>el.querySelector(s),urlIn=$("#jh-url"),fileIn=$("#jh-file"),go=$("#jh-run"),msg=$("#jh-msg"),out=$("#jh-out");
  const UPLOAD_API="https://clooud.my.id/uploder/";
  const BLACK_API="https://api.xyurei.biz.id/api/edit/jadihitam?url=";
  const say=(t,bad=false)=>{msg.textContent=t;msg.className=bad?"err":"hint"};
  const findUrl=(obj)=>{
   const found=[];
   const walk=(v,k="",d=0)=>{
    if(d>7||v==null)return;
    if(typeof v==="string"){
     if(/^https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+$/i.test(v.trim()) && /url|link|src|file|result|download|image/i.test(k+" result"))found.push(v.trim());
     else if(/^https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+$/i.test(v.trim()))found.push(v.trim());
     return;
    }
    if(Array.isArray(v)){v.forEach(x=>walk(x,k,d+1));return}
    if(typeof v==="object")Object.keys(v).forEach(key=>walk(v[key],key,d+1));
   };
   walk(obj);return found[0]||"";
  };
  const uploadFile=async file=>{
   const fd=new FormData();fd.append("file",file,file.name);
   const r=await fetch(UPLOAD_API,{method:"POST",body:fd});
   const raw=await r.text();let data;try{data=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{data=raw}
   if(!r.ok)throw new Error("Upload gagal (HTTP "+r.status+").");
   const link=typeof data==="string"?(data.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/)||[])[0]||"":findUrl(data);
   if(!link)throw new Error("Upload merespons, tetapi URL file tidak ditemukan. Respons: "+raw.slice(0,500));
   return link;
  };
  go.addEventListener("click",async()=>{
   if(go.disabled)return;
   const typed=urlIn.value.trim(),file=fileIn.files&&fileIn.files[0];
   if(!typed&&!file){say("Masukkan URL gambar atau pilih file terlebih dahulu.",true);return}
   if(typed){try{const u=new URL(typed);if(!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol))throw 0}catch{say("URL tidak valid. Gunakan URL gambar http atau https.",true);return}}
   go.disabled=true;out.replaceChildren();
   try{
    let imageUrl=typed;
    if(!imageUrl&&file){say("Mengunggah gambar untuk mendapatkan URL…");imageUrl=await uploadFile(file)}
    say("Memproses gambar…");
    const r=await fetch(BLACK_API+encodeURIComponent(imageUrl));
    const raw=await r.text();let data;try{data=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{data=raw}
    if(!r.ok)throw new Error("API Jadi Hitam gagal (HTTP "+r.status+").");
    const resultUrl=typeof data==="string"?(data.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/)||[])[0]||"":findUrl(data);
    if(!resultUrl){
     const pre=document.createElement("pre");pre.className="out";pre.style.whiteSpace="pre-wrap";pre.textContent=typeof data==="string"?data:JSON.stringify(data,null,2);
     out.appendChild(pre);say("API merespons, tetapi URL hasil belum dikenali. Periksa respons di bawah.",true);return;
    }
    const box=document.createElement("div");box.className="stack";
    const img=document.createElement("img");img.src=resultUrl;img.alt="Hasil gambar jadi hitam";img.loading="lazy";img.style.cssText="display:block;width:100%;max-height:420px;object-fit:contain;border:2px solid var(--bd);border-radius:14px;background:var(--bg)";
    img.onerror=()=>{img.style.display="none"};
    const link=document.createElement("a");link.href=resultUrl;link.target="_blank";link.rel="noopener noreferrer";link.className="out";link.textContent=resultUrl;
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="row";
    const cp=document.createElement("button");cp.className="btn";cp.type="button";cp.textContent="Salin URL";cp.onclick=()=>copy(resultUrl);
    const open=document.createElement("a");open.className="btn alt";open.href=resultUrl;open.target="_blank";open.rel="noopener noreferrer";open.textContent="Buka hasil";
    row.append(cp,open);box.append(img,link,row);out.appendChild(box);say("Berhasil! Gambar hasil sudah siap.");
   }catch(e){say(e.message||"Terjadi kesalahan saat memproses gambar.",true)}
   finally{go.disabled=false}
  });
 }
});

/* ============ File Uploader — Clooud API ============ */
TOOLS.push({
 id:"file-uploader",name:"File Uploader",desc:"Unggah berbagai jenis file dan dapatkan tautan langsung untuk dibagikan.",cat:"Utilities",featured:false,
 icon:'<path d="M12 16V4"/><path d="m7 9 5-5 5 5"/><path d="M4 16v4h16v-4"/>',
 render(el){
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="panel">
   <div class="f">
    <label for="fu-file">Pilih file</label>
    <input id="fu-file" type="file">
   </div>
   <div id="fu-picked" class="hint">Belum ada file dipilih.</div>
   <button class="btn" id="fu-go" type="button">Upload file</button>
   <div id="fu-msg" class="hint" role="status" aria-live="polite">Pilih file apa pun untuk mulai mengunggah.</div>
   <div id="fu-out"></div>
  </div>`;
  const $=s=>el.querySelector(s),input=$("#fu-file"),picked=$("#fu-picked"),go=$("#fu-go"),msg=$("#fu-msg"),out=$("#fu-out");
  const API="https://clooud.my.id/uploder/";
  const say=(t,bad=false)=>{msg.textContent=t;msg.className=bad?"err":"hint"};
  const findUrl=obj=>{
   const urls=[];
   const walk=(v,d=0)=>{
    if(d>8||v==null)return;
    if(typeof v==="string"){const m=v.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/i);if(m)urls.push(m[0]);return}
    if(Array.isArray(v)){v.forEach(x=>walk(x,d+1));return}
    if(typeof v==="object"){
     const keys=Object.keys(v).sort((a,b)=>Number(/url|link|download|result/i.test(b))-Number(/url|link|download|result/i.test(a)));
     keys.forEach(k=>walk(v[k],d+1));
    }
   };
   walk(obj);return urls[0]||"";
  };
  input.addEventListener("change",()=>{
   const f=input.files&&input.files[0];
   picked.textContent=f?f.name+" · "+fmtB(f.size)+(f.type?" · "+f.type:""):"Belum ada file dipilih.";
   out.replaceChildren();say(f?"File siap diunggah.":"Pilih file untuk mulai.");
  });
  go.addEventListener("click",async()=>{
   const file=input.files&&input.files[0];if(!file){say("Pilih file terlebih dahulu.",true);return}
   go.disabled=true;out.replaceChildren();say("Mengunggah "+file.name+"…");
   try{
    const fd=new FormData();fd.append("file",file,file.name);
    const r=await fetch(API,{method:"POST",body:fd});
    const raw=await r.text();let data;try{data=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{data=raw}
    if(!r.ok)throw new Error("Upload gagal (HTTP "+r.status+").");
    const link=typeof data==="string"?(data.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/)||[])[0]||"":findUrl(data);
    if(!link){
     const pre=document.createElement("pre");pre.className="out";pre.style.whiteSpace="pre-wrap";pre.textContent=typeof data==="string"?data:JSON.stringify(data,null,2);out.appendChild(pre);
     throw new Error("File terkirim, tetapi format URL dari server belum dikenali. Respons API ditampilkan di atas.");
    }
    const box=document.createElement("div");box.className="stack";
    const a=document.createElement("a");a.className="out";a.href=link;a.target="_blank";a.rel="noopener noreferrer";a.textContent=link;
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="row";
    const cp=document.createElement("button");cp.className="btn";cp.type="button";cp.textContent="Salin URL";cp.onclick=()=>copy(link);
    const open=document.createElement("a");open.className="btn alt";open.href=link;open.target="_blank";open.rel="noopener noreferrer";open.textContent="Buka file";
    row.append(cp,open);box.append(a,row);out.appendChild(box);say("Upload berhasil! URL file siap digunakan.");
   }catch(e){say(e.message||"Terjadi kesalahan saat upload.",true)}
   finally{go.disabled=false}
  });
 }
});

const state={cat:"Semua",q:"",scroll:0};
const bodies=new Map();            // cache DOM tools agar state tidak hilang
const byId=id=>TOOLS.find(t=>t.id===id);
const esc=s=>s.replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));

function chips(){
 $("#chips").innerHTML=CATS.map(c=>`<button class="chip" aria-pressed="${c===state.cat}" data-c="${c}">${c}</button>`).join("");
}
function list(){
 const q=state.q.trim().toLowerCase();
 const res=TOOLS.filter(t=>(state.cat==="Semua"||t.cat===state.cat)&&(!q||(t.name+" "+t.desc+" "+t.cat).toLowerCase().includes(q)));
 $("#grid").innerHTML=res.map((t,i)=>`<button class="tile t${i%4} enter" data-id="${t.id}"><span class="ic">${ico(t.icon)}</span><span><b>${esc(t.name)}</b><p>${esc(t.desc)}</p></span></button>`).join("");
 const none=!TOOLS.length;
 $("#none").classList.toggle("hide",!none);$("#empty").classList.toggle("hide",res.length>0||none);
 $("#tools .search").classList.toggle("hide",none);$("#chips").classList.toggle("hide",none);
 $("#listTitle").textContent=(state.cat==="Semua"&&!q?"Semua tools":"Hasil")+" ("+res.length+")";
 $("#listTitle").classList.toggle("hide",!res.length);
}
function home(){
 document.body.classList.remove("chatmode");
 $("#view").classList.add("hide");$("#home").classList.remove("hide");
 document.title="Xaerisoft — Tools buatan Wilverse";
 scrollTo(0,state.scroll);
}
function open(t){
 if(!$("#home").classList.contains("hide"))state.scroll=scrollY;
 document.body.classList.toggle("chatmode",t===CHAT);
 $("#home").classList.add("hide");const v=$("#view");v.classList.remove("hide");v.classList.add("enter");
 $("#vIcon").innerHTML=ico(t.icon);$("#vName").textContent=t.name;$("#vDesc").textContent=t.desc;
 const host=$("#vBody");host.textContent="";
 let b=bodies.get(t.id);
 if(!b){b=document.createElement("div");try{t.render(b)}catch(e){b.innerHTML='<div class="err">Tools gagal dimuat.</div>';console.error(e)}bodies.set(t.id,b)}
 host.appendChild(b);b.start&&b.start();document.title=t.name+" — Xaerisoft";scrollTo(0,0);$("#back").focus({preventScroll:true});
}
function dockSet(){const h=location.hash,k=h==="#chat"?"chat":h==="#announcement"?"announcement":h==="#wil-ai"?"wil-ai":/^#(profil|pengaturan|cs|admin)$/.test(h)?"profil":"home";document.querySelectorAll(".dock [data-t]").forEach(b=>b.dataset.t===k?b.setAttribute("aria-current","page"):b.removeAttribute("aria-current"))}
function route(){
 dockSet();
 const m=location.hash.match(/^#tool-([\w-]+)$/),t=PAGES[location.hash]||(m&&byId(m[1]));
 if(t)open(t);else home();if(t!==CHAT){const c=bodies.get("chat");c&&c.stop&&c.stop()}
 if(!t&&/^#(tools|berita)$/.test(location.hash))$("#"+location.hash.slice(1)).scrollIntoView();
}
document.addEventListener("click",e=>{
 const c=e.target.closest("[data-id]");if(c){location.hash="tool-"+c.dataset.id;return}
 const ch=e.target.closest("[data-c]");if(ch){state.cat=ch.dataset.c;chips();list();}
});
$("#back").onclick=()=>{const h=location.hash;location.hash=(h==="#pengaturan"||h==="#cs"||h==="#admin")?"profil":h==="#announcement"?"tools":"tools"};
$("#q").addEventListener("input",e=>{state.q=e.target.value;list()});

addEventListener("hashchange",route);

function wxHTML(){const g=GEO,w=g.w;
 if(g.s==="ok"&&w){const x=WX[w.c]||["Cuaca","cloud"],k=!w.day&&(x[1]==="sun"||x[1]==="psun")?"moon":x[1];
  return`<div class="wl">${ico(WI.pin)}<b>${esc(g.place||"Lokasi terdeteksi")}</b><button class="wr" type="button" data-k="geo" aria-label="Perbarui cuaca">${ico(WI.ref)}</button></div><div class="wm"><span class="wi">${ico(WI[k])}</span><div class="wt">${Math.round(w.t)}°</div><div class="wd"><b>${x[0]}</b><span>${Math.round(w.lo)}° / ${Math.round(w.hi)}°</span></div></div><div class="wf"><span>Terasa<b>${Math.round(w.f)}°</b></span><span>Lembap<b>${w.h}%</b></span><span>Angin<b>${Math.round(w.wd)} km/j</b></span></div>`}
 if(g.s==="ask")return`<div class="wl">${ico(WI.pin)}<b>Mencari lokasi…</b></div><p class="wn">Izinkan akses lokasi di jendela yang muncul untuk melihat cuaca.</p>`;
 return`<div class="wl">${ico(WI.pin)}<b>Cuaca belum tersedia</b></div><p class="wn">${g.s==="denied"?"Izin lokasi ditolak, jadi cuaca belum bisa tampil. Aktifkan lagi lewat pengaturan situs di browser.":"Lokasi belum bisa dibaca."}</p>${g.s==="denied"?"":'<button class="btn alt wb" type="button" data-k="geo">Coba lagi</button>'}`}
async function getWx(la,lo){const j=await(await fetch(`https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=${la.toFixed(3)}&longitude=${lo.toFixed(3)}&current=temperature_2m,apparent_temperature,relative_humidity_2m,weather_code,wind_speed_10m,is_day&daily=temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min&forecast_days=1&timezone=auto`)).json(),c=j.current;if(!c)throw 0;
 return{t:c.temperature_2m,f:c.apparent_temperature,h:c.relative_humidity_2m,c:c.weather_code,wd:c.wind_speed_10m,day:c.is_day,hi:j.daily.temperature_2m_max[0],lo:j.daily.temperature_2m_min[0]}}
async function getPlace(la,lo){const u=(v,i,a)=>v&&a.indexOf(v)===i;
 try{const j=await(await fetch(`https://api.bigdatacloud.net/data/reverse-geocode-client?latitude=${la}&longitude=${lo}&localityLanguage=id`)).json(),a=[j.city||j.locality,j.principalSubdivision].filter(u);if(a.length)return{place:a.join(", "),cc:j.countryCode||""}}catch{}
 try{const j=await(await fetch(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/reverse?format=jsonv2&zoom=10&accept-language=id&lat=${la}&lon=${lo}`)).json(),d=j.address||{},a=[d.city||d.town||d.village||d.county||d.suburb,d.state].filter(u);if(a.length)return{place:a.join(", "),cc:(d.country_code||"").toUpperCase()}}catch{}
 return{place:la.toFixed(2)+", "+lo.toFixed(2),cc:""}}
async function geoStart(){
 if(!navigator.geolocation||!isSecureContext){GEO.s="na";kepo();return}
 let st="prompt";try{st=(await navigator.permissions.query({name:"geolocation"})).state}catch{}
 if(st==="denied"){GEO.s="denied";GEO.w=null;kepo();return}
 if(st==="granted"&&GEO.s!=="ok")try{const c=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("xa-geo")||"null");if(c&&c.w){Object.assign(GEO,{s:"ok",w:c.w,place:c.place,cc:c.cc});kepo()}}catch{}
 if(GEO.s!=="ok"){GEO.s="ask";kepo()}
 navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(async p=>{
  try{const[w,pl]=await Promise.all([getWx(p.coords.latitude,p.coords.longitude),getPlace(p.coords.latitude,p.coords.longitude)]);
   Object.assign(GEO,{s:"ok",w,place:pl.place,cc:pl.cc});try{localStorage.setItem("xa-geo",JSON.stringify({w,place:pl.place,cc:pl.cc}))}catch{}}
  catch{if(GEO.s!=="ok")GEO.s="fail"}kepo()},
 e=>{if(e.code===1){GEO.s="denied";GEO.w=null;try{localStorage.removeItem("xa-geo")}catch{}}else if(GEO.s!=="ok")GEO.s="fail";kepo()},
 {timeout:15000,maximumAge:6e5})}
function tick(){const t=$("#kt");if(!t)return;
 t.textContent=new Date().toLocaleTimeString("id-ID",{hour:"2-digit",minute:"2-digit",second:"2-digit"}).replace(/\./g,":");
 const on=navigator.onLine;$("#ko").innerHTML=`<span class="${on?"up":"down"}">${on?"Online":"Offline"}</span>`;$("#kd").style.background=on?"":"var(--no)";
 $("#kb").textContent=batt?Math.round(batt.level*100)+"%"+(batt.charging?" (mengisi)":""):"—"}
function kepo(){const u=XA().user,r=GEO.cc||regionOf(),nm=u?(u.displayName||(u.isAnonymous?"Tamu":(u.email||"Pengguna").split("@")[0])):"Tamu";
 $("#kepo").innerHTML=`<div class="kh"><div class="kavw"><div class="kav">${u?avaInner(u,nm):esc(nm[0].toUpperCase())}</div><i class="kdot" id="kd"></i></div><div class="kinfo"><div class="ktop"><small>${greet()}</small>${u?'<a class="kbtn" href="#profil">Profil</a>':'<button class="kbtn pri" type="button" data-k="in">Masuk</button>'}</div><b>${nameHTML(nm,u&&u.uid)}</b><span class="kmeta">${r?`<i class="kcc">${esc(r)}</i><span>${esc(cname(r))}</span>`:"<span>Negara tidak diketahui</span>"}<i class="ksep"></i><span>${DEV}</span></span></div></div><div class="kw" aria-live="polite">${wxHTML()}</div><div class="kt"><div><span>Jam</span><b id="kt"></b></div><div><span>Browser</span><b>${BRW}</b></div><div><span>Baterai</span><b id="kb">—</b></div><div><span>Status</span><b id="ko"></b></div></div>`;
 $("#dkacc span").textContent=u?"Profil":"Masuk";tick()}
const signOut=()=>{const x=XA();x.out&&x.out().then(()=>location.reload())};
