/* Berita politik (RSS publik lewat rss2json) */
const FEEDS=[["Antara","https://www.antaranews.com/rss/politik.xml"],["CNN Indonesia","https://www.cnnindonesia.com/nasional/rss"]];
const ago=ts=>{const m=Math.round((ts-Date.now())/6e4),f=new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat("id",{numeric:"auto"});return Math.abs(m)<60?f.format(m,"minute"):Math.abs(m)<1440?f.format(Math.round(m/60),"hour"):f.format(Math.round(m/1440),"day")};
async function news(){const box=$("#news");let items=null;
 try{const c=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem("xa-news")||"null");if(c&&Date.now()-c.t<6e5)items=c.i}catch{}
 if(!items){box.innerHTML='<div class="nsk"></div>'.repeat(3);
  const rs=await Promise.allSettled(FEEDS.map(([n,u])=>fetch("https://api.rss2json.com/v1/api.json?rss_url="+encodeURIComponent(u)).then(r=>r.json()).then(j=>{if(j.status!=="ok")throw 0;return j.items.map(i=>({s:n,t:i.title,l:i.link,i:i.thumbnail||(i.enclosure&&i.enclosure.link)||"",d:Date.parse((i.pubDate||"").replace(" ","T")+"Z")||0}))})));
  items=rs.filter(r=>r.status==="fulfilled").flatMap(r=>r.value).filter(i=>/^https?:/.test(i.l)).sort((a,b)=>b.d-a.d).slice(0,24);
  if(items.length)try{sessionStorage.setItem("xa-news",JSON.stringify({t:Date.now(),i:items}))}catch{}}
 if(!items.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="empty" style="width:100%"><b>Berita belum bisa dimuat</b>Periksa koneksi lalu coba lagi.<br><button class="btn alt" id="nr">Coba lagi</button></div>';$("#nr").onclick=news;return}
 box.innerHTML=items.map(i=>`<a class="nc" href="${esc(i.l)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${/^https?:/.test(i.i)?`<img class="nimg" src="${esc(i.i)}" alt="" loading="lazy" onerror="this.remove()">`:""}<div class="b"><b>${esc(i.t)}</b><small>${esc(i.s)}${i.d?" · "+ago(i.d):""}</small></div></a>`).join("")}
$("#nav2").onclick=e=>{const d=e.target.closest("[data-d]")?.dataset.d;d&&$("#news").scrollBy({left:d*290,behavior:"smooth"})};

<script>
(()=>{
/*PURE_START*/
const h=v=>String(v??"").replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c]));
const inl=t=>t.replace(/`([^`\n]+)`/g,"<code>$1</code>").replace(/\*\*([^*\n]+)\*\*/g,"<strong>$1</strong>").replace(/(^|[\s(])(https?:\/\/[^\s<)]+)/g,'$1<a href="$2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">$2</a>');
const md=src=>{
 const out=[],re=/```[\w+#.-]*\n?([\s\S]*?)```/g;let last=0,m;
 const para=s=>{let ul=null;const fl=()=>{if(ul){out.push("</"+ul+">");ul=null}};
  for(const raw of s.split("\n")){const l=raw.trimEnd();let k;
   if((k=l.match(/^\s*[-*•]\s+(.*)$/))){if(ul!=="ul"){fl();out.push("<ul>");ul="ul"}out.push("<li>"+inl(h(k[1]))+"</li>")}
   else if((k=l.match(/^\s*\d+[.)]\s+(.*)$/))){if(ul!=="ol"){fl();out.push("<ol>");ul="ol"}out.push("<li>"+inl(h(k[1]))+"</li>")}
   else if((k=l.match(/^#{1,4}\s+(.*)$/))){fl();out.push("<p><strong>"+inl(h(k[1]))+"</strong></p>")}
   else if(!l.trim())fl();
   else{fl();out.push("<p>"+inl(h(l))+"</p>")}}
  fl()};
 while((m=re.exec(src))){para(src.slice(last,m.index));out.push('<pre><button type="button" class="wa-cp" data-cp>Salin</button><code>'+h(m[1].replace(/\n$/,""))+"</code></pre>");last=re.lastIndex}
 para(src.slice(last));return out.join("")};
const u16=(b,o)=>b[o]|b[o+1]<<8,u32=(b,o)=>(b[o]|b[o+1]<<8|b[o+2]<<16|b[o+3]<<24)>>>0;
async function zipEntry(buf,name){
 const b=new Uint8Array(buf);let e=-1;
 for(let i=b.length-22;i>=Math.max(0,b.length-70000);i--)if(u32(b,i)===0x06054b50){e=i;break}
 if(e<0)throw new Error("Bukan file .docx yang valid.");
 const n=u16(b,e+10);let p=u32(b,e+16);
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
  if(u32(b,p)!==0x02014b50)break;
  const method=u16(b,p+10),cs=u32(b,p+20),nl=u16(b,p+28),el=u16(b,p+30),cl=u16(b,p+32),lo=u32(b,p+42);
  const nm=new TextDecoder().decode(b.subarray(p+46,p+46+nl));
  if(nm===name){
   const ds=lo+30+u16(b,lo+26)+u16(b,lo+28),data=b.subarray(ds,ds+cs);
   if(method===0)return data;
   if(method!==8)throw new Error("Kompresi .docx tidak didukung.");
   return new Uint8Array(await new Response(new Blob([data]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream("deflate-raw"))).arrayBuffer())}
  p+=46+nl+el+cl}
 return null}
async function docxText(buf){
 const d=await zipEntry(buf,"word/document.xml");if(!d)throw new Error("Isi .docx tidak ditemukan.");
 return new TextDecoder().decode(d).replace(/<w:tab\/>/g,"\t").replace(/<w:br\/>/g,"\n").replace(/<\/w:p>/g,"\n").replace(/<[^>]+>/g,"")
  .replace(/&lt;/g,"<").replace(/&gt;/g,">").replace(/&quot;/g,'"').replace(/&apos;/g,"'").replace(/&amp;/g,"&").replace(/\n{3,}/g,"\n\n").trim()}
/*PURE_END*/

const SUG=["Bantu debug kode gue","Kasih ide proyek web baru","Jelasin konsep rumit dengan simpel","Bikinin caption yang nendang"];
const x=()=>window.XA||{};
const say=m=>{try{toast(m)}catch{}};

const uidKey=()=>KEY+((x().user&&x().user.uid)||"anon");
const store={
 load(){try{const d=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(uidKey())||"null");if(d&&Array.isArray(d.c))return d}catch{}return{c:[],a:""}},
 save(S){for(let i=0;i<6;i++){try{localStorage.setItem(uidKey(),JSON.stringify(S));return true}catch{if(S.c.length<2)return false;S.c.pop()}}return false}};
async function readAtt(f){
 const name=f.name||"file",type=f.type||"",o={name,type,size:f.size};
 if(f.size>MAXSIZE)return{...o,kind:"big"};
 if(/\.docx$/i.test(name)){const t=await docxText(await f.arrayBuffer());return{...o,kind:"text",text:t.slice(0,MAXF),cut:t.length>MAXF}}
 if(type.startsWith("text/")||type==="application/json"||type==="application/xml"||TEXT_EXT.test(name)){const t=(await f.text()).replace(/\u0000/g,"");return{...o,kind:"text",text:t.slice(0,MAXF),cut:t.length>MAXF}}
 return{...o,kind:"media",url:type.startsWith("image/")?URL.createObjectURL(f):""}}

  const fit=()=>{input.style.height="auto";input.style.height=Math.min(input.scrollHeight,120)+"px"};
  const chipsHtml=(arr,rm)=>arr.map((f,i)=>{const warn=f.kind!=="text";const im=f.url?`<img src="${h(f.url)}" alt="">`:"";return `<span class="wa-chip${warn?" warn":""}" title="${warn?"Belum bisa dibaca API Wil AI":"Isi file dikirim ke Wil AI"}">${im}<span>${h(f.name)}</span>${rm?`<button type="button" data-rm="${i}" aria-label="Hapus lampiran">×</button>`:""}</span>`}).join("");

  async function send(text){

  async function addFiles(list){
   for(const f of [...list]){

    catch(e){setSt("Gagal membaca "+f.name+": "+(e.message||"file rusak"))}}
   renderFiles()}

    return}

   if((b=t.closest("[data-sg]"))){send(b.dataset.sg);return}
   if((b=t.closest("[data-cp]"))){const code=b.parentElement.querySelector("code");copyText(code?code.textContent:"");return}
   if((b=t.closest("[data-act]"))){const a=b.dataset.act,c=active();
    if(a==="copy"&&c&&c.m[+b.dataset.i])copyText(c.m[+b.dataset.i].x);

    return}

   if((b=t.closest("[data-del]"))){if(busy){say("Tunggu Wil AI selesai dulu");return}const id=b.dataset.del;S.c=S.c.filter(c=>c.id!==id);if(S.a===id)S.a="";save();renderLog();renderDrawer()}});
  /* ---- keyboard ponsel: kolom ketik tetap terlihat ---- */
  const vv=window.visualViewport,docEl=document.documentElement;
  const clearKb=()=>{document.body.classList.remove("wai-kb");docEl.style.removeProperty("--wai-h")};
  const kb=()=>{if(location.hash!=="#wil-ai"||!vv){return}

  /* ---- siklus halaman ---- */
  el.start=()=>{document.body.classList.add("wil-ai-active");load();renderLog();renderDrawer();renderFiles();setTimeout(kb,50)};

 }

 /* ===== Wil AI: dipanggil langsung dari browser, tanpa backend dan tanpa Firebase ===== */
 const WIL_URL="https://api.izzxd.my.id/api/ai/aichat",WIL_MAXURL=12000;
 const WIL_BASE={provider:"gemini",model:"pro",system:WIL_SYSTEM,deepthink:"false",research:"false",code:"false",apikey:"izzxd-86cpjw6"};
 let wilLast=0;
 const wilQS=p=>new URLSearchParams({prompt:p,...WIL_BASE}).toString();
 const wilReq=async(prompt,ms,post)=>{const c=new AbortController(),t=setTimeout(()=>c.abort(),ms||60000);
  try{const q=wilQS(prompt);
   const r=post?await fetch(WIL_URL,{method:"POST",headers:{"Content-Type":"application/x-www-form-urlencoded"},body:q,signal:c.signal}):await fetch(WIL_URL+"?"+q,{signal:c.signal});
   let d;try{d=await r.json()}catch{throw new Error("Respons server Wil AI bukan JSON yang valid (HTTP "+r.status+").")}
   if(!r.ok)throw new Error(d?.message||d?.error||("Server Wil AI merespons HTTP "+r.status+"."));
   if(d?.status!==true)throw new Error(d?.message||d?.error||"API Wil AI menolak permintaan. Kredit API mungkin habis.");
   const reply=String(d?.result?.reply??"").trim();if(!reply)throw new Error("Wil AI menerima respons kosong dari server.");
   return {reply,provider:d?.result?.provider,model:d?.result?.model,credits:d?.result?.creditsRemaining}}
  catch(e){if(e?.name==="AbortError")throw new Error("Wil AI terlalu lama merespons. Coba lagi sebentar.");if(e instanceof TypeError)throw new Error("Tidak bisa terhubung ke API Wil AI (jaringan putus atau diblokir CORS).");throw e}
  finally{clearTimeout(t)}};
 const wilCall=(prompt,ms)=>wilReq(prompt,ms,false);
 /* Permintaan pendek lewat GET (sesuai dokumentasi API). Yang panjang (isi file) lewat POST; kalau server menolak POST, jatuh ke GET dengan isi file dipotong. */
 const wilAsk=async({head="",files="",question=""},ms)=>{
  const full=head+files+question;
  if(wilQS(full).length<=WIL_MAXURL)return wilReq(full,ms||70000,false);
  try{return await wilReq(full,ms||70000,true)}
  catch(e){if(!files||/terlalu lama/.test(e.message))throw e;
   let f=files;while(f.length>200&&wilQS(head+f+question).length>WIL_MAXURL)f=f.slice(0,Math.floor(f.length*.7));
   const r=await wilReq(head+f+"\n[...file dipotong]\n"+question,ms||70000,false);r.truncated=true;return r}};
 const wilGlobal=async(text,u,id)=>{const q=String(text).replace(/@wil[\s_-]*ai\b[\s,:]*/ig,"").trim()||"halo";
  const say=m=>{try{toast(m)}catch{}};
  if(Date.now()-wilLast<8000){say("Wil AI masih mikir, tunggu sebentar ya");return}
  wilLast=Date.now();say("Wil AI lagi mikir…");
  const nm=u.displayName||"Pengguna";
  let reply;try{reply=(await wilCall("Chat Global Xaerisoft. "+nm+" bilang: "+q,45000)).reply}catch(er){console.warn("Wil AI Global:",er.message);say("Wil AI gagal: "+er.message);return}
  try{await Fs.addDoc(col,{text:reply.slice(0,1500),uid:"wil-ai",name:"Wil AI",photo:"",at:Fs.serverTimestamp(),replyTo:{id,uid:u.uid,name:String(nm).slice(0,40),text:q.slice(0,120)}})}
  catch(er){console.warn("Wil AI Global tulis:",er.code||er.message);say("Wil AI gagal kirim ke chat: "+(er.code||er.message))}};
 const usernameKey=async n=>{const normalized=String(n??"").normalize("NFKC").trim().toLowerCase();if(!normalized)throw new Error("Nama tidak boleh kosong.");const digest=await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256",new TextEncoder().encode(normalized));return "v2_"+Array.from(new Uint8Array(digest),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join("")};
 const claimUsername=async(uid,name)=>{const display=String(name??"").trim();if(!display)throw new Error("Nama tidak boleh kosong.");const key=await usernameKey(display),newRef=Fs.doc(db,"usernames",key),userRef=ud(uid);await Fs.runTransaction(db,async tx=>{const us=await tx.get(userRef),oldKey=us.exists()?(us.data().usernameKey||""):"";const ns=await tx.get(newRef);let oldSnap=null,oldRef=null;if(oldKey&&oldKey!==key){oldRef=Fs.doc(db,"usernames",oldKey);oldSnap=await tx.get(oldRef)}if(ns.exists()&&ns.data().uid!==uid)throw new Error("Nama sudah dipakai akun lain. Coba nama berbeda.");tx.set(newRef,{uid,name:display,normalizedName:display.normalize("NFKC").toLowerCase(),updatedAt:Fs.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});if(oldRef&&oldSnap&&oldSnap.exists()&&oldSnap.data().uid===uid)tx.delete(oldRef);tx.set(userRef,{name:display,nameLower:display.normalize("NFKC").toLowerCase(),usernameKey:key,usernameKeyVersion:2,updatedAt:Fs.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true})});return key;};
 const adminUid=uid=>!!uid&&(uid===ROLE_CONFIG.ownerUid||ROLE_CONFIG.adminUids.includes(uid));
 Au.getRedirectResult(auth).catch(e=>{console.error("Auth redirect error:",e)});

  social:async p=>{
   const pr=p==="github"?new Au.GithubAuthProvider():new Au.GoogleAuthProvider();

   return Au.signInWithRedirect(auth,pr);

  out:()=>Au.signOut(auth),

  setPhoto:async d=>{const cu=auth.currentUser;await Fs.setDoc(ud(cu.uid),{photo:d,name:cu.displayName||"",at:Fs.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});P[cu.uid]={...P[cu.uid],photo:d};fire()},
  photo:id=>{if(id==="wil-ai")return "";const p=P[id];if(p===undefined&&!Q.has(id)){Q.add(id);Fs.getDoc(ud(id)).then(x=>{const d=x.exists()?x.data():{};P[id]=d;try{if(typeof d.photo==="string"&&d.photo&&d.photo.length<=100000)localStorage.setItem("xa-pp:"+id,d.photo);else if(!d.photo)localStorage.removeItem("xa-pp:"+id)}catch(e){}}).catch(()=>{setTimeout(()=>Q.delete(id),8000)}).then(()=>fire())}
   if(p&&typeof p.photo==="string"&&p.photo)return p.photo;
   try{return localStorage.getItem("xa-pp:"+id)||""}catch(e){return ""}},
  pReady:id=>P[id]!==undefined,
  send:async(t,o)=>{const u=auth.currentUser;if(!u)throw new Error("Masuk dulu.");const d={text:t,uid:u.uid,name:u.displayName||(u.isAnonymous?"Tamu":"Pengguna"),photo:u.photoURL||"",at:Fs.serverTimestamp()},r=o&&o.replyTo,f=o&&o.fwd;
   if(r&&typeof r.id==="string"&&r.id)d.replyTo={id:r.id,uid:String(r.uid||""),name:String(r.name||"").slice(0,40),text:String(r.text||"").slice(0,120)};

  react:(id,k)=>Fs.updateDoc(Fs.doc(db,"messages",id),{["reactions."+auth.currentUser.uid]:k}),

  reactAnn:(id,k)=>Fs.updateDoc(Fs.doc(db,"announcements",id),{["reactions."+auth.currentUser.uid]:k}),

  listenAnnouncements:(cb,er)=>Fs.onSnapshot(Fs.query(annCol,Fs.orderBy("at","desc"),Fs.limit(50)),s=>cb(s.docs.map(d=>({id:d.id,...d.data()}))),er),
  enablePush:async()=>{const u=auth.currentUser;if(!u)throw new Error("Masuk dulu untuk mengaktifkan notifikasi.");if(!("Notification" in window)||!("serviceWorker" in navigator))throw new Error("Browser tidak mendukung notifikasi.");if(/^ISI_/.test(FCM_VAPID_KEY)||!/^[-A-Za-z0-9_]{80,}$/.test(FCM_VAPID_KEY))throw new Error("Isi FCM_VAPID_KEY dari Firebase Console terlebih dahulu.");const perm=await Notification.requestPermission();if(perm!=="granted")throw new Error("Izin notifikasi tidak diberikan.");const [Msg]=await Promise.all([import(B+"firebase-messaging.js")]);const messaging=Msg.getMessaging(app);Msg.onMessage(messaging,p=>{const n=p.notification||{};if(Notification.permission==="granted")new Notification(n.title||"Update Xaerisoft",{body:n.body||"Ada pengumuman baru.",icon:n.icon||"/favicon.ico",image:n.image})});const reg=await navigator.serviceWorker.register("/firebase-messaging-sw.js");const token=await Msg.getToken(messaging,{vapidKey:FCM_VAPID_KEY,serviceWorkerRegistration:reg});if(!token)throw new Error("Token notifikasi tidak tersedia.");await Fs.setDoc(Fs.doc(tokenCol,u.uid),{uid:u.uid,token,updatedAt:Fs.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});return token},
  role:id=>{if(!id)return roleOf();XA.photo(id);return roleOf(id,P[id])},
